{"batchcomplete":true,"query":{"pages":[{"pageid":56175506,"ns":0,"title":"UFC 229","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2018}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC 229: Khabib vs. McGregor\n|image= UFC 229 Poster.png\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2018|10|06}}\n|venue= [[T-Mobile Arena]]\n|city= [[Paradise, Nevada]], United States\n|attendance= 20,034{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/ufc-229-draws-20034-fans-17-million-gate|title=UFC 229 draws 20,034 fans, falls just shy of live gate record|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-07}}\n|gate=$17,188,894.67{{cite web|url=http://boxing.nv.gov/results/Top_MMA_Gates|title=Top MMA Gates|date=September 16, 2016|publisher=Nevada State Athletic Commission}}\n|buyrate= 2,400,000{{cite web |last1=Simon |first1=Zane |title=Report - UFC 229: Khabib vs. McGregor hits new record high of 2.5 million PPV buys |url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/10/11/17964814/ufc-229-khabib-nurmagomedov-vs-conor-mcgregor-ppv-buy-record-mma-two-half-million-new-high-mma-news |website=Bloody Elbow |access-date=6 February 2021 |archive-date=5 October 2020 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201005155825/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/10/11/17964814/ufc-229-khabib-nurmagomedov-vs-conor-mcgregor-ppv-buy-record-mma-two-half-million-new-high-mma-news |url-status=dead }}\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Santos vs. Anders]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Volkan vs. Smith]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 229: Khabib vs. McGregor''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC) that was held on October 6, 2018. The fight took place between [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] and [[Conor McGregor]] at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/ufc-announces-2018-schedule-september-through-end-of-year|title=UFC announces rest of 2018 schedule from September through end of year|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-07-08|access-date=2018-07-09}}\n\nThis event was one of the most anticipated MMA events of all time, and is the most-bought UFC (and MMA) pay-per-view of all time with a buyrate of 2.4 million.\n\n==Background==\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 320\n | image1 = Khabib Nurmagomedov 2.png\n | width1 = 2800| height1 = 2900\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = Conor McGregor 2018.jpg\n | width2 = 2600 | height2 = 2900\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer = Going into the fight [[Khabib Nurmagomedov|Nurmagomedov]] ''(left)'' had an undefeated record of 26–0, while [[Conor McGregor|McGregor]] ''(right)'' was the first fighter to hold titles in two divisions simultaneously. The fight was dubbed \"the biggest in UFC history\" by organization president [[Dana White]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/45069013|title=Conor McGregor v Khabib Nurmagomedov the biggest fight in UFC history – White|publisher=bbc.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-04|access-date=2018-08-05}}\n}}\n\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between the undefeated current [[List of UFC champions|UFC Lightweight champion]] [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] and former [[UFC Featherweight Championship|featherweight]] and [[UFC Lightweight Championship|lightweight]] champion [[Conor McGregor]].{{Cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Khabib-vs-McGregor-set-for-October-6|title=Khabib vs McGregor set for October 6|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-03|access-date=2018-08-05}} McGregor captured the lightweight title at [[UFC 205]], when he was the reigning featherweight champion.{{cite news|url=http://www.latimes.com/sports/la-live-coverage-ufc-205-mcgregor-vs-alvarez-20161110-htmlstory.html|title=UFC 205: Conor McGregor claims lightweight title with knockout of Eddie Alvarez|work=[[Los Angeles Times]]|author=Todd Martin|date=2016-11-13|access-date=2018-08-05}} McGregor never defended the title, he took some time off in 2017 due to the birth of his firstborn and later venturing himself in a [[Floyd Mayweather Jr. vs. Conor McGregor|boxing match]] against [[Floyd Mayweather Jr.]]{{cite news|url=https://www.forbes.com/sites/mattconnolly/2016/11/13/conor-mcgregor-ufc-205-announcements-time-off-for-newborn-ufc-ownership-demand-and-an-apology/#624ccdc55b6d|title=Conor McGregor's UFC 205 announcements: time off for newborn, UFC ownership demand and an 'apology'|work=forbes.com|author=Matt Connolly|date=2016-11-13|access-date=2018-08-05}}{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/live/boxing/40874881|title=Mayweather beats McGregor – as it happened|publisher=bbc.com|author=Michael Emons|date=2017-08-26|access-date=2018-08-05}} The UFC originally planned an interim title bout between Nurmagomedov and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]] welterweight winner [[Tony Ferguson]] at [[UFC 209]], but it was canceled due to Khabib having medical issues related to his weight cut on weigh-in day.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/3/3/14805818/ufc-releases-statement-on-khabib-nurmagomedov|title=UFC releases statement on Khabib Nurmagomedov|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-03-03|access-date=2018-08-05}} Ferguson eventually won the interim title at [[UFC 216]] against [[Kevin Lee]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/10/ufc-216-results-tony-ferguson-taps-resilient-kevin-lee-in-third-to-win-interim-lightweight-title|title=UFC 216 results: Tony Ferguson taps resilient Kevin Lee in 3rd to win interim lightweight title|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2017-10-08|access-date=2018-08-05}} Ferguson was then expected to meet Nurmagomedov at [[UFC 223]], with the winner being crowned the undisputed champion (as McGregor would be stripped of the title as soon as the bout took place). In turn, it was scrapped once again (fourth time) as Ferguson got injured, and after several possibilities for an opponent, Nurmagomedov eventually won the vacant title against [[Al Iaquinta]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/4/1/17186764/tony-ferguson-out-of-ufc-223-max-holloway-to-step-in-and-face-khabib-nurmagomedov-lightweight-title|title=Tony Ferguson out of UFC 223, Max Holloway to step in and face Khabib Nurmagomedov for lightweight title|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2018-04-01|access-date=2018-08-05}}{{Cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2018/04/ufc-223-results-khabib-nurmagomedov-dominates-al-iaquinta-lightweight-champion-conor-mcgregor|title=UFC 223 results: Khabib Nurmagomedov dominates Al Iaquinta to become new lightweight champ|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2018-04-08|access-date=2018-08-05}} The event was preceded by a widely publicized [[UFC 223#Conor McGregor and team assault on bus|incident]] in which McGregor threw a [[Hand truck|dolly]] at a bus carrying Nurmagomedov and other fighters, injuring two. He was arrested and charged, but the UFC did not formally discipline him. Footage of the incident was later used in official promotion, sparking controversy, though UFC President [[Dana White]] defended its inclusion.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/4/5/17203162/conor-mcgregor-crew-allegedly-cause-chaotic-scene-after-ufc-223-media-day|title=Conor McGregor, crew cause chaotic scene after UFC 223 media day|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2018-04-05|access-date=2018-08-05}}{{Cite web |date=2018-10-10 |title=Opinion: How silence in the face of Conor McGregor's Islamophobia led to a disgraceful mass brawl after his defeat |url=https://www.independent.co.uk/voices/conor-mcgregor-vs-khabib-nurmagomedov-ufc-mma-fight-racism-islamophobia-a8574526.html |access-date=2025-07-11 |website=The Independent |language=en}}\n\n[[Jussier Formiga]] was previously scheduled to face [[Sergio Pettis]] in January 2017 at [[UFC Fight Night: Rodríguez vs. Penn]]. However, Formiga pulled out of the fight for undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/john-moraga-in-for-formiga-vs-sergio-pettis-drakkar-klose-meets-devin-powell-at-ufc-fight-night-103|title=John Moraga in for Formiga vs. Sergio Pettis at UFC Fight Night 103|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-29|access-date=2018-08-02}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{cite news|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/curtinhas-jussier-formiga-encara-sergio-pettis-no-ufc-229-em-las-vegas.ghtml|title=Jussier Formiga faces Sergio Pettis at UFC 229 in Las Vegas|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-02|access-date=2018-08-02|language=pt}}\n\nOn October 1, [[Sean O'Malley (fighter)|Sean O'Malley]] announced that he was pulled from a bout against [[José Alberto Quiñónez]] after failing a [[US Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) drug test.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/9/30/17921592/sean-omalley-announces-hes-failed-usada-drug-test-out-of-ufc-229|title=Sean O'Malley announces he's failed USADA drug test, out of UFC 229|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2018-09-30|access-date=2018-10-01}} In turn, promotion officials elected to remove Quiñónez from the card and reschedule him for a future event.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/ufc-229-new-start-time-holtzman-vs-patrick-fs1-prelims-khabib-conor|title=UFC 229 gets new start time, Scott Holtzman vs. Alan Patrick bumped to FS1 prelims|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-03|access-date=2018-10-03}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Lightweight|[[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] Championship|access-date=2018-08-13}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nThe following fighters received $50,000 bonuses:{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/ufc-229-bonuses-ferguson-pettis-pick-up-extra-50k-each-for-their-brawl-before-the-other-brawl|title=UFC 229 bonuses: Ferguson, Pettis pick up extra $50k each for their brawl before the other brawl|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-07}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Tony Ferguson vs. Anthony Pettis'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Derrick Lewis''' and '''Aspen Ladd'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $6,636,000.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/ufc-229-salaries-conor-mcgregor-khabib-nurmagomedov-derrick-lewis-brawl|title=UFC 229 salaries: McGregor makes $3 million, Khabib $2 million – eventually – on $6.6 million payroll|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-07}}\n\n* '''Khabib Nurmagomedov''': $2,000,000 (no win bonus) def. Conor McGregor: $3,000,000\n* '''Tony Ferguson''': $250,000 (includes $50,000 win bonus) def. Anthony Pettis: $100,000\n* '''Dominick Reyes''': $100,000(includes $45,000 win bonus) def. Ovince Saint Preux: $86,000\n* '''Derrick Lewis''': $270,000 (includes $135,000 win bonus) def. Alexander Volkov: $75,000\n* '''Michelle Waterson''': $100,000 (includes $50,000 win bonus) def. Felice Herrig: $40,000\n* '''Jussier Formiga''': $86,000 (includes $43,000 win bonus) def. Sergio Pettis: $46,000\n* '''Vicente Luque''': $76,000 (includes $38,000 win bonus) def. Jalin Turner: $10,000\n* '''Aspen Ladd''': $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Tonya Evinger: $30,000\n* '''Scott Holtzman''': $60,000 (includes $30,000 win bonus) def. Alan Patrick: $30,000\n* '''Yana Kunitskaya''': $50,000 (includes $25,000 win bonus) def. Lina Länsberg: $20,000\n* '''Nik Lentz''': $100,000 (includes $50,000 win bonus) def. Grey Maynard: $54,000\n* '''Tony Martin''': $56,000 (includes $28,000 win bonus) def. Ryan LaFlare: $33,000\n\n==Records set==\nAt the T-Mobile Arena, the event drew an attendance of 20,034 fans, generating a [[live gate]] revenue of {{US$|17.2 million|long=no}}. It set the record for the highest ever mixed martial arts attendance and live gate in [[Nevada]]. It also set the record for the biggest MMA [[pay-per-view]] event, with 2.4{{nbsp}}million buys in the United States.\n\n==Viewership==\nIn Nurmagomedov's home country of [[Russia]], the fight set the viewership record for the country's most-watched MMA event. On Russian channel [[Match TV]], where the event began airing at 05:00 [[Moscow Time|MSK]] and the Nurmagomedov-McGregor fight after 07:00 MSK, the fight was watched by more than 4{{nbsp}}million viewers and reached a peak viewership rating of 67.5% for the time slot in the capital [[Moscow]].{{cite news|url=https://matchtv.ru/boxing/matchtvnews_NI905532_Match_TV_stal_liderom_sredi_vseh_kanalov_v_Moskve_vo_vrema_boja_Nurmagomedov__Makgregor|title=\"МАТЧ ТВ\" СТАЛ ЛИДЕРОМ СРЕДИ ВСЕХ КАНАЛОВ В МОСКВЕ ВО ВРЕМЯ БОЯ НУРМАГОМЕДОВ – МАКГРЕГОР\n|trans-title=Match TV Became the Leader Among All Channels in Moscow During the Battle of Nurmagomedov – McGregor|publisher=[[Match TV]]|date=2018-10-08|access-date=2018-10-15|language=ru}} In the [[United Kingdom]], the fight was watched by 1,282,500 viewers on [[pay television]] subscription channel [[BT Sport 1]], including 260,700 viewers for the live 01:00 broadcast and 1,021,800 viewers for the 06:00 replay.{{cite web|url=https://www.barb.co.uk/viewing-data/four-screen-dashboard/|title=Weekly top programmes on four screens|publisher=[[Broadcasters' Audience Research Board]]|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-15}}\n\n==Nurmagomedov–McGregor post-fight controversy==\nSeconds after the fight, Nurmagomedov hurled his mouthpiece towards McGregor’s corner, then proceeded to climb out of the octagon and charged toward McGregor's cornerman [[Dillon Danis]]. Soon after, McGregor and [[Abubakar Nurmagomedov]] (cousin of Khabib) also tried to climb out of the octagon, but a scuffle broke out between them.{{Cite news|url=https://www.joe.co.uk/sport/khabib-nurmagomedov-cousin-conor-mcgregor-punch-203121|title=Khabib's cousin Abubakar Nurmagomedov shows damage inflicted on him by Conor McGregor |publisher=joe.co.uk|author=Patrick McCarry|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-07}} Back in the octagon, McGregor was then attacked by two of Nurmagomedov's cornermen: [[Zubaira Tukhugov]] and Esed Emiragaev.{{Cite news|url=https://sport.business-gazeta.ru/article/228337|title=McGregor was attacked after the battle. Who are these people?|publisher=sport.business-gazeta.ru|author=Ruslan Vasilyev|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-07|language=ru}}\n\nAs a result of the incident, Nurmagomedov's payment for the fight was withheld by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC) pending an investigation into his actions. Tukhugov was scheduled to fight on October 27 at [[UFC Fight Night: Volkan vs. Smith]] against [[Artem Lobov]], McGregor's teammate who was confronted by Nurmagomedov in April 2018.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/report-zubaira-tukhugov-speaks-out-after-slapping-conor-mcgregor-as-promised|title=Zubaira Tukhugov speaks out after slapping Conor McGregor 'as promised'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-08}}\n\nNurmagomedov appeared at the post-fight interview and apologized to the NSAC, saying he was provoked by the McGregor team's [[trash-talk]] targeting his family, nation, and religion, as well as the [[UFC 223]] bus incident.{{Cite news|url=https://www.cbc.ca/sports/ufc-229-conor-mcgregor-khabib-nurmagomedov-1.4853516|title=Post-match mayhem breaks out after Nurmagomedov's win over McGregor at UFC 229|publisher=cbc.ca|author=The Associated Press|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-12}}\n\nThe NSAC filed a formal complaint against both McGregor and Nurmagomedov and the final hearing was initially scheduled to take place in November.{{Cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24933080/conor-mcgregor-khabib-nurmagomedov-face-nsac-complaints-brawl|title=Khabib, Conor both facing complaints after brawl|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2018-10-09|access-date=2018-10-09}} On October 12, they announced both fighters received a suspension for ten days come October 15 and a hearing would be held on October 24.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/conor-mcgregor-khabib-nurmagomedov-temporary-suspensions-nsac-ufc-229|title=Conor McGregor, Khabib Nurmagomedov receive initial 10-day suspensions from NSAC|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-11|access-date=2018-10-12}} On October 24, the NSAC unanimously voted to release half of Nurmagomedov's payout immediately.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/khabib-nurmagomedov-receives-1-million-of-ufc-229-purse|title=NSAC releases $1 million of Khabib Nurmagomedov's UFC 229 purse|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2018-10-24|access-date=2018-10-25}} The commission also voted to issue temporary suspensions for both fighters and ordered them to appear in person for a further hearing in December to resolve the case.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/conor-mcgregor-khbaib-temporary-suspensions-extended-resolution-in-december|title=NSAC extends temporary suspensions for McGregor, Khabib; resolution expected in December|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco and John Morgan|date=2018-10-24|access-date=2018-10-25}}\n\nOn January 29, 2019, the NSAC announced a nine-month suspension for Nurmagomedov and a $500,000 fine. The agreement also includes a potential suspension reduction of up to three months if he delivers to the commission a public service announcement on anti-bullying. To receive the reduction, the commission must approve the PSA and its distribution plan. Additionally, Nurmagomedov can only receive credit based on the time he submits the PSA. McGregor was suspended for six months and given a $50,000 fine. In separate agreements, Abubakar Nurmagomedov and Tukhugov were each suspended one year for their roles in the melee. All suspensions are retroactive to the day of the event.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/khabib-nurmagomedov-conor-mcgregor-suspension-ufc-229-brawl-nsac|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov, Conor McGregor suspended for UFC 229 brawl|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2019-01-29|access-date=2019-01-30}} A few days later, Nurmagomedov stated that he would not opt to do the PSA stating \"The state of Nevada is where drugs, prostitution and gambling are officially permitted. Let them work on themselves.\"{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/02/khabib-nurmagomedov-says-no-to-ufc-229-bullying-psa-for-nevada-drugs-prostitution-gambling-legal|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov nixes PSA, says Nevada is where drugs, prostitution, gambling are legal|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2019-02-05|access-date=2019-02-05}} Danis was suspended for seven months and fined $7,500, also retroactive to the day of the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/02/dillon-danis-suspended-seven-months-fined-7500-for-his-role-in-ufc-229-brawl|title=Dillon Danis suspended seven months, fined $7,500 for his role in UFC 229 brawl|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2019-02-25|access-date=2019-02-25}} On May 22, 2019, NSAC reduced the suspensions by 35 days, which allowed Abubakar Nurmagomedov and Tukhugov to be eligible to compete again on September 1, 2019.{{Cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/26801923|title=NSAC reduces brawl ban for Khabib's teammates|date=2019-05-22|website=ESPN.com|language=en|access-date=2019-05-23}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2018 in UFC]]\n*[[List of current UFC [[The Forum (Inglewood, California)|The Forum]] in [[Inglewood, California]].{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/ufc-announces-2018-schedule-september-through-end-of-year|title=UFC announces news|url=https://www.mmanytt.com/ufc/ufc-announces-remaining-schedule-for-2018-including-return-to-madison-square-garden|title=UFC announces remaining schedule for 2018 including return to Madison Square Garden|publisher=mmanytt.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2018-07-08|access-date=2018-07-09}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between former champion [[Jon Jones]] and former title challenger [[Alexander Gustafsson]] headlined this event.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/jon-jones-vs-alexander-gustafsson-rematch-headlines-ufc-232-for-light-heavyweight-title-daniel-cormier|title=Jon Jones vs. Alexander Gustafsson 2 set for UFC 232; Daniel Cormier to be stripped of title|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2018-10-10|access-date=2018-10-10}} As the current divisional champion [[Daniel Cormier]] focused on defending his [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]], the promotion indicated that he would be stripped of his title and that the winner of the proposed Jones/Gustafsson bout would become the undisputed champion. Jones and Gustafsson previously fought in September of 2013 at [[UFC 165]] where Jones defended his title via unanimous decision.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2013/09/ufc-165-results-and-photos-jon-jones-vs-alexander-gustafsson|title=UFC 165 results and photos: Champ Jon Jones survives gutsy Alexander Gustafsson|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-22|access-date=2018-10-10}} The rematch was previously booked to headline [[UFC 178]] in September 2014, but the pairing was scrapped after Gustafsson tore his right meniscus and lateral collateral ligament.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-178-at-mgm-grand|title=UFC 178 set for Sept 27 at MGM Grand|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-07-22|date=2014-07-22}}{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Jones-Gustafsson-2-Postponed-DC-gets-Title-Shot|title=Jones-Gustafsson 2 postponed; DC gets title shot|author=Thomas Gerbasi|publisher=ufc.com|access-date=2014-07-23|date=2014-07-23}} A day before the event, Cormier relinquished his title as he said \"I'd rather walk away this way, than have the history books say I was stripped\".{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/daniel-cormier-relinquishes-light-heavyweight-title-ufc-232-jon-jones-dana-white|title=Daniel Cormier relinquishes light heavyweight title ahead of UFC 232|author=Mike Bohn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|access-date=2018-12-28|date=2018-12-28}}\n\nThe event was originally expected to take place at [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]]. However on December 23, the event was moved to [[The Forum (Inglewood, California)|The Forum]] in [[Inglewood, California]] due to a Jones' drug testing abnormality stemming from earlier that month. The test found a trace amount of [[turinabol]] in Jones' system, the same substance he tested positive for in 2017 that led to the 15-month suspension he recently was cleared from in order to fight this week. Because the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] does not have proper time to investigate in order to keep Jones cleared to fight in Las Vegas, the UFC decided to move the entire event to Inglewood as the [[California State Athletic Commission]] would grant Jones a licence. UFC [[Vice president|VP]] of Athlete Health and Performance [[Jeff Novitzky]] said [[USADA]], in consultation with other organizations, believes the test is a \"pulsing\" effect and not a new ingestion of the substance that he tested positive for after [[UFC 214]] in July 2017.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/jon-jones-drug-test-atypical-result-ufc-232-moved-to-los-angeles-gustafsson-dana-white|title=Jon Jones drug test nets 'atypical finding,' UFC 232 moved to Los Angeles on one week's notice|author=Matt Erickson and John Morgan|publisher=mmajunkie.com|access-date=2018-12-23|date=2018-12-23}}\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 320\n | image1 = Cristiane \"Cyborg\" Santos (40618979464).jpg\n | width1 = 2600 | height1 = 2900\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = Amanda Nunes.jpg\n | width2 = 2600 | height2 = 2900\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer = Cyborg ''(left)'' had a record of 20–1 (1), with her only loss dating back to 2005 when she made her professional debut. Meanwhile, Nunes ''(right)'' had three title defenses and a 16–4 record. This was the first time in UFC history that two current female champions faced each other.\n}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Cris Cyborg]] (who is also a former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|Strikeforce Women's Featherweight Champion]] and [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC Featherweight Champion]]) and former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Amanda Nunes]] took place at the event.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/08/cris-cyborg-vs-amanda-nunes-booked-ufc-232|title=Cris Cyborg vs. Amanda Nunes champion-vs.-champion fight set for UFC 232|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-22|access-date=2018-08-22}} This was the fourth time in UFC history that champions in different divisions fought for the same title, following [[UFC 94]], [[UFC 205]] and [[UFC 226]]. \n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Sijara Eubanks]] and [[Jessica Eye]] was expected to take place at this event, but Eubanks was pulled from the bout on October 2 in favor of a headliner for the vacant [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] against former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Valentina Shevchenko (fighter)|Valentina Shevchenko]] at [[UFC 230]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmaonpoint.com/news/2018/8/31/jessica-eye-vs-sijara-eubanks-targeted-for-ufc-232|title=Jessica Eye vs. Sijara Eubanks targeted for UFC 232|publisher=mmaonpoint.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-31|access-date=2018-10-04|archive-date=2018-10-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181004225918/https://www.mmaonpoint.com/news/2018/8/31/jessica-eye-vs-sijara-eubanks-targeted-for-ufc-232|url-status=dead}}{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/ufc-230-main-event-valentina-shevchenko-vs-sijara-eubanks-vacant-flyweight-title-joanna-jedrzejczyk|title=UFC 230 finally has main event: Valentina Shevchenko vs. Sijara Eubanks for vacant flyweight title|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-02|access-date=2018-10-02}} However, that fight was canceled a week later.{{Cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24943833/daniel-cormier-derrick-lewis-headline-ufc-230|title=Daniel Cormier-Derrick Lewis official; Valentina Shevchenko moved back to UFC 231|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2018-10-09|access-date=2018-10-09}} Eubanks remained on the card at [[UFC 230]] and fought against former title challenger [[Roxanne Modafferi]], while Eye found a new opponent at [[UFC 231]].{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/sijara-eubanks-will-fight-at-ufc-230-roxanne-modafferi|title=Sijara Eubanks will fight at UFC 230, after all|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-10|access-date=2018-10-10}}\n\n[[Tom Duquesnoy]] was expected to face [[Nathaniel Wood (fighter)|Nathaniel Wood]] at the event. However, Duquesnoy pulled out of the fight on November 12 citing a rib injury.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/11/12/18088260/tom-duquesnoy-out-of-ufc-232-bout-with-nathaniel-wood|title=Tom Duquesnoy out of UFC 232 bout with Nathaniel Wood|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2018-11-12|access-date=2018-11-12}} Wood then faced [[Andre Ewell]].{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/andre-ewell-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-tegen-nathaniel-wood-tijdens-ufc-232-in-las-vegas/|title=Andre Ewell tackles short notice fight against Nathaniel Wood during UFC 232 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-11-30|access-date=2018-11-30|language=nl}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Brian Kelleher]] and [[Montel Jackson]] was initially slated to take place at UFC 230. However the fight was cancelled on the day of the event due to a medical issue for Kelleher and was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite news|url=https://mymmanews.com/brian-kelleher-montel-jackson-rebooked/|title=Brian Kelleher vs. Montel Jackson rebooking slated for UFC 232|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2018-11-14|access-date=2018-11-14}} At the weigh-ins, Jackson weighed in at 137 lb, 1 pound over the bantamweight non-title fight limit of 136 lb. He was fined 20% of his fight purse, and Kelleher proceeded at catchweight.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/ufc-232-jon-jones-alexander-gustafsson-cris-cyborg-amanada-nunes-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC 232 weigh-in results: One fighter misses despite three-hour window|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-12-28|access-date=2018-12-28}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Jon Jones]]\n|def.\n|[[Alexander Gustafsson]]\n|KO (punches)\n|3\n|2:02\n|For the vacant [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Featherweight\n|[[Amanda Nunes]] \n|def.\n|[[Cris Cyborg]] (c)\n|KO (punch)\n|1\n|0:51\n|For the [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Michael Chiesa]]\n|def.\n|[[Carlos Condit]]\n|Submission (kimura)\n|2\n|0:56\n| \n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]]\n|def.\n|[[Ilir Latifi]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Alexander Volkanovski]]\n|def. \n|[[Chad Mendes]] \n|TKO (punches)\n|2\n|4:14\n| \n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (Fox Sports 1)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Walt Harris (fighter)|Walt Harris]]\n|vs.\n|[[Andrei Arlovski]]\n|No Contest (overturned)\n|3\n|5:00\n|Originally a split decision (27-30, 29-28, 29-28) win for Harris; overturned after he tested positive for [[LGD-4033]]\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Featherweight\n|[[Megan Anderson (fighter)|Megan Anderson]]\n|def.\n|[[Cat Zingano]] \n|TKO (eye injury)\n|1\n|1:01\n| \n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Petr Yan]]\n|def.\n|[[Douglas Silva de Andrade]] \n|TKO (corner stoppage)\n|2\n|5:00\n| \n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Ryan Hall (grappler)|Ryan Hall]]\n|def. \n|[[B.J. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $3,506,000.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/ufc-232-salaries-cyborg-nunes-jones-gustafsson-arlovski-penn|title=UFC 232 salaries: 10 fighters take home six-figure paydays|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-12-30|access-date=2018-12-30}}\n\n* '''Jon Jones:''' $500,000 (no win bonus) def. Chad Mendes: $87,000\n* '''Walt Harris:''' $72,000 ($36,000 win bonus; fined 4,000) def. Andrei Arlovski: $300,000\n* '''Megan Anderson:''' $60,000 ($30,000 win bonus) def. Brian Kelleher: $27,000\n\n* ''Montel Jackson was fined $4,000, 20 percent of his purse for failing to make the required weight for his fight with Brian Kelleher. That money will be issued to Kelleher.''\n\n==Aftermath==\n\nNunes became the first woman to be champion in two divisions simultaneously and third fighter overall (after [[Conor McGregor]] at [[UFC 205]] and [[Daniel Cormier]] at [[UFC 226]]), as well as the sixth person overall to win a title in different divisions.\n\nOn January 23, 2019, it was reported that [[Walt Harris (fighter)|Walt Harris]] failed a fight-night drug test and was temporarily suspended by the CSAC. He tested positive for [[LGD-4033]], a muscle-wasting medication in the same family of selective androgen receptor modulators (SARMs) like [[ostarine]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/walt-harris-temporarily-suspended-csac-positive-ufc-232-drug-test|title=Walt Harris temporarily suspended by CSAC after positive UFC 232 drug test|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2019-01-23|access-date=2019-01-24}} On February 28, Harris was suspended for four months (retroactive to the day of the fight) and fined $4,000 by the CSAC for the failed drug test, as he was able prove that he ingested the substance via a tainted supplement. His fight against former [[UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Andrei Arlovski]] was overturned to a no contest.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/2/28/18245516/walt-harris-suspended-four-months-by-csac-for-positive-drug-test-ufc-232-win-overturned|title=Walt Harris suspended four months by CSAC for positive drug test, UFC 232 win overturned|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2019-02-28|access-date=2019-03-01}}\n\n==See also==\n* [[List of UFC events]]\n* [[2018 in UFC]]\n* news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/7/17/17583202/ufc-argentina-debut-buenos-aires-november|title=UFC set for Argentina debut in Buenos Aires on Nov. 17|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-07-17|access-date=2018-07-17}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's first visit to [[Argentina]], the 22nd country.\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Neil Magny]] and [[Santiago Ponzinibbio]] headlined the event.{{cite web|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/neil-magny-deixa-luta-em-sp-e-deve-encarar-ponzinibbio-no-ufc-argentina.ghtml|title=Neil Magny leaves fight in SP to face Ponzinibbio in UFC November|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-08-23|access-date=2018-08-23}}\n\n[[Veronica Macedo]] was expected to face [[Maryna Moroz]] at the event. However, Macedo pulled out of the fight due to an injury and was replaced by [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Women's flyweight championship|KSW Women's Flyweight Champion]] and promotional newcomer [[Ariane Lipski]].{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/ksw-champ-ariane-lipski-vs-maryna-moroz-flyweight-ufc-fight-night-140-buenos-aires|title=KSW champ Ariane Lipski meets Maryna Moroz at UFC Fight Night 140 in Buenos Aires|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn\t|date=2018-09-23|access-date=2018-09-24}} In turn, Moroz pulled out due to injury on October 30 and as a result the bout was cancelled.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/10/29/18039806/maryna-moroz-injured-out-ufc-argentina-ariane-lipski|title=Maryna Moroz injured, out of UFC Argentina bout with Ariane Lipski|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-10-30|access-date=2018-10-30}}{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/10/31/18049728/with-no-opponent-available-ariane-lipski-pulled-from-ufc-argentina|title=With no opponent available, Ariane Lipski pulled from UFC Argentina card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-10-31|access-date=2018-11-08}}\n\n[[Claudio Puelles]] was scheduled to face [[Devin Powell]] at the event. However, Puelles pulled out of the bout in mid-October with an undisclosed injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Jesus Pinedo]].{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/jesus-pinedo-maakt-ufc-debuut-tegen-devin-powell-tijdens-ufc-buenos-aires/|title=Jesus Pinedo makes UFC debut against Devin Powell during UFC Buenos Aires|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-10-17|access-date=2018-10-17|language=nl}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Jared Cannonier]] and [[Alessio Di Chirico]] was scheduled to the event. However, on October 20, it was reported that Cannonier would meet former [[WSOF Middleweight Championship|WSOF Middleweight]] and [[WSOF Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[David Branch (fighter)|David Branch]] at [[UFC 230]] instead. Initially, it was not sure if a replacement will be sought from the UFC. Ultimately, Di Chirico was pulled from the card.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/jacare-david-branch-vs-jared-cannonier-middleweight-ufc-230|title=With 'Jacare' shifted to co-main event, David Branch gets Jared Cannonier at UFC 230|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2018-10-20|access-date=2018-10-20}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America 2]] lightweight winner [[Enrique Barzola]] was expected to face [[Nad Narimani]] at the event. However, on October 26, it was reported that Barzola pulled out of the bout due to an arm injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer Sergio Giglio.{{Cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/enrique-barzola-geblesseerd-vecht-niet-tegen-nad-narimani-tijdens-ufc-buenos-aires/|title=Enrique Barzola injured, not fighting Nad Narimani at UFC Buenos Aires|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-10-26|access-date=2018-10-26|language=nl}}{{Cite web|url=https://trome.pe/deportes/ufc/ufc-peruano-sergio-giglio-firma-compania-estara-ufc-argentina-ingles-nad-narimani-101964|title=Peruvian fighter Sergio Giglio signed with UFC and will face Englishman Nad Narimani in Argentina|publisher=trome.pe|author=Staff|date=2018-10-28|access-date=2018-10-28|language=es|archive-date=2018-10-28|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181028151825/https://trome.pe/deportes/ufc/ufc-peruano-sergio-giglio-firma-compania-estara-ufc-argentina-ingles-nad-narimani-101964|url-status=dead}} In turn on November 8, it was reported that Giglio was forced out due to injury and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Anderson dos Santos (fighter)|Anderson dos Santos]].{{Cite news|url=https://mma-today.com/nad-narimani-faces-berinja-buenos-aires|title=Nad Narimani gets opponent change, faces 'Berinja' in Buenos Aires|publisher=mma-today.com|author=Max Freedman|date=2018-11-08|access-date=2018-11-09}}{{Dead link|date=July 2025 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=yes }}\n\n[[Tom Breese]] was expected to face [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil]] middleweight winner [[Cezar Ferreira]] at the event. However, on November 9, it was announced that he pulled out of the event due to injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Ian Heinisch]].{{Cite news|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/breese-deixa-luta-contra-mutante-no-ufc-argentina-e-ian-heinisch-e-escolhido-como-substituto.ghtml|title=Breese pulls out of Mutante bout at UFC Argentina and Ian Heinisch is chosen as his replacement|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone|date=2018-11-09|access-date=2018-11-09|language=pt}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Cynthia Calvillo]] weighed in at 118 lb, 2 pounds over the strawweight non-title fight limit of 116 lb. She was fined 20 percent of her purse, which went to her opponent [[Poliana Botelho]] and the bout proceeded at catchweight.{{Cite Championship]] that was held on August 25, 2018 at [[Pinnacle Bank Arena]] in [[Lincoln, Nebraska]], United States.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2018/04/ufc-debuts-in-boise-lincoln-among-additions-to-summer-schedule|title=UFC debuts in Boise, Lincoln among additions to summer schedule|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-04-21|access-date=2018-06-14}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe show was the UFC’s third visit to Nebraska, with the previous two events contested in [[Omaha]].\n\nA lightweight bout between former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]] [[Justin Gaethje]] and [[Al Iaquinta]] was expected to serve as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/06/justin-gaethje-al-iaquinta-headliner-other-bouts-official-ufc-fight-night-135-lincoln|title=Justin Gaethje vs. Al Iaquinta headliner, other bouts official for UFC Fight Night 135 in Lincoln|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-06-14|access-date=2018-06-14}} However on June 28, it was announced that Iaquinta withdrew from the bout and was replaced by [[James Vick]].{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/06/james-vick-replaces-al-iaquinta-meets-justin-gaethje-ufc-fight-night-135-lincoln-mma|title=James Vick replaces Al Iaquinta, meets Justin Gaethje in new UFC Lincoln headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2018-06-28|access-date=2018-06-28}}\n\n[[Bryan Barberena]] was expected to face [[Jake Ellenberger]] at [[UFC Fight Night: Rivera vs. Moraes]]. However, Barberena pulled out of the fight citing a leg injury and the bout was scrapped.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/03/bryan-barberena-injured-new-ufc-fight-night-131-opponent-sought-for-jake-ellenberger|title=Bryan Barberena injured; new UFC Fight Night 131 opponent sought for Jake Ellenberger|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-03-23|access-date=2018-07-18}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/bryan-barberena-jake-ellenberger-ufc-fight-night-135-lincoln-nebraska|title=Bryan Barberena announces UFC Lincoln bout with Jake Ellenberger|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2018-07-18|access-date=2018-07-18}}\n\n[[Alexa Grasso]] was expected to face former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Angela Hill]] at the event, but pulled out on July 19 due to a knee spasm.{{cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24138992/alexa-grasso-forced-withdraw-fight-angela-hill|title=Alexa Grasso forced to withdraw from fight with Angela Hill|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2018-07-19|access-date=2018-07-19}} She was replaced by [[Cortney Casey]].{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/cortney-casey-replaces-alexa-grasso-vs-angela-hill-ufc-lincoln|title=Cortney Casey replaces Alexa Grasso, meets Angela Hill at UFC Lincoln|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2018-07-19|access-date=2018-07-19}}\n\n[[Antônio Braga Neto]] was expected to face [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia]] light heavyweight winner [[Andrew Sanchez]] at the event. However, Neto was removed from the bout on August 2 due to undisclosed personal issues and replaced by [[Markus Perez]].{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/markus-perez-vervangt-antonio-braga-neto-tegen-andrew-sanchez-tijdens-ufc-lincoln/|title=Markus Perez replaces Antonio Braga Neto against Andrew Sanchez at UFC Fight Night: Hunt vs. Oleinik\n|image= UFC Fight Night 136 Poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2018|09|15}}\n|venue=[[Olympic Stadium (Moscow)|Olimpiyskiy Stadium]]\n|city=[[Moscow]], [[Russia]]\n|attendance= 22,603{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/ufc-fight-night-136-moscow-russia-22603-attendance-live-gate-record|title=UFC Fight Night 136 draws reported 22,603 attendance for $1.84 million live gate|publisher=mmajunkie|author=Staff|date=2018-09-15|access-date=2018-09-16}}\n|gate= $1,840,000\n|previousevent=[[UFC 228|UFC 228: Woodley vs. Till]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Santos vs. Anders]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Hunt vs. Oleinik''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 136''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on September 15, 2018 at [[Olympic Stadium (Moscow)|Olimpiyskiy Stadium]] in [[Moscow]], [[Russia]].{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/05/ufc-fight-night-moscow-marks-promotions-debut-in-russia-on-sept-15|title=UFC Fight Night Moscow marks promotion's debut in Russia on Sept. 15|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2018-05-16|access-date=2018-05-16}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event marked the promotion's first visit to Russia, the 21st country. The UFC arranged to use half of the venue and used a modified seating configuration for the arena.{{cite news|url=https://www.sport-express.ru/martial/mma/news/ufc-optimiziroval-ceny-na-bilety-na-pervyy-turnir-v-rossii-1416002/|title=UFC optimized ticket prices for the first event in Russia|publisher=sport-express.ru|author=Radik Khuzin|date=2018-06-01|access-date=2018-06-01|language=ru}}\n\nWhile not officially announced, the promotion was initially targeting a heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Fabrício Werdum]] and [[Aleksei Oleinik]] to serve as the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/5/21/17377926/fabricio-werdum-vs-alexey-oleynik-set-to-headline-ufc-moscow-fight-card|title=Fabricio Werdum vs. Alexey Oleynik set to headline UFC Moscow|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2018-05-21|access-date=2018-05-21}} However, on May 22, Werdum was flagged for a potential USADA doping violation and his status moving forward became unknown.{{Cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Fabricio-Werdum-Facing-USADA-AntiDoping-Violation-for-Failed-OutofCompetition-Drug-Test-137073|title=Fabricio Werdum facing USADA anti-doping violation for failed out-of-competition drug test|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2018-05-22|access-date=2018-05-22}} On July 19, the promotion announced that a bout between the [[K-1 World Grand Prix 2001 Final|2001 K-1 World Grand Prix]] winner and former interim title challenger [[Mark Hunt]] and Oleinik would serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=http://www.mmamad.com/mark-hunt-vs-alexey-oleynik-headlines-ufc-fight-night-136-in-russia/|title=Mark Hunt vs Alexey Oleynik headlines UFC Fight Night 136 in Russia|publisher=mmamad.com|author=Dale Jordan|date=2018-07-19|access-date=2018-07-19}}{{dead link|date=September 2025|bot=medic}}{{cbignore|bot=medic}} On September 11, USADA announced that Werdum was suspended for two years due to steroid [[trenbolone]] and its metabolite epitrenbolone.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/usada-fabricio-werdum-suspended-two-years-for-steroid|title=USADA: Fabricio Werdum suspended two years for steroid|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2018-09-11|access-date=2018-09-12}}\n\n[[Rustam Khabilov]] was expected to face [[Kajan Johnson]] at [[UFC Fight Night: Werdum vs. Volkov]]. However, Khabilov pulled out of the fight citing an injury and the bout was scrapped.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/02/rustam-khabilov-injured-kajan-johnson-london-ufc-fight-night-127|title=Rustam Khabilov says he's off London's UFC Fight Night 127 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-02-08|access-date=2018-08-08}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmasucka.com/2018/08/08/ufc-moscow-khabilov-vs-kajan-johnson/|title=Rustam Khabilov vs Kajan Johnson rebooked For UFC Moscow|author=Shakiel Mahjouri|publisher=mmasucka.com|date=2018-08-08|access-date=2018-08-08}}\n\n[[Douglas Silva de Andrade]] was briefly scheduled to face [[Petr Yan]] at the event. However, Andrade pulled out of the fight on August 9 citing a foot injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/8/9/17670094/douglas-silva-de-andrade-injured-out-ufc-moscow-fight-petr-yan|title=Douglas Silva de Andrade injured, out of UFC Moscow fight with Petr Yan|author=Guilherme Cruz|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2018-08-09|access-date=2018-08-09}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer Jin Soo Son.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/08/petr-yan-vs-jin-soo-son-bantamweight-ufc-fight-night-136-moscow|title=Petr Yan meets replacement opponent Jin Soo Son at UFC Moscow|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-18|access-date=2018-08-18}}\n\n[[Krzysztof Jotko]] was expected to face promotional newcomer Adam Yandiev at the event. However, Jotko pulled out of the fight on August 16 citing injury and was replaced by [[Jordan Johnson (fighter)|Jordan Johnson]].{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/jordan-johnson-vervangt-de-geblesseerde-krzysztof-jotko-tegen-adam-yandiev-in-moskou/|title=Jordan Johnson replaces the injured Krzysztof Jotko against Adam Yandiev in Moscow|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-08-17|access-date=2018-08-17|language=nl}}\n\n[[Cláudio Silva]] was scheduled to face [[Ramazan Emeev]] at the event. However, Silva pulled out of the fight in early September citing a lower back injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/9/3/17814364/claudio-silva-injured-ufc-moscow-fight-ramazan-emeev|title=Claudio Silva injured, out of UFC Moscow fight with Ramazan Emeev|author=Guilherme Cruz|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2018-09-03|access-date=2018-09-03}} Emeev instead faced promotional newcomer Stefan Sekulić.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/ufc-debutant-stefan-sekulic-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-tegen-ramazan-emeev-in-moskou/|title=UFC debutant Stefan Sekulic tackles short notice fight against Ramazan Emeev in Moscow|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-09-07|access-date=2018-09-07|language=nl}}\n\n[[Omari Akhmedov]] was expected to face [[C. B. Dollaway]] at the event. However, Akhmedov pulled out of the fight in early September and was briefly replaced by promotional newcomer Artem Frolov.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/m-1-global-kampioen-artem-frolov-maakt-ufc-debuut-tegen-cb-dollaway-in-moskou/|title=M-1 Global Champion Artem Frolov makes UFC debut against CB Dollaway in Moscow|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-09-04|access-date=2018-09-04|language=nl}} However, after just three days, Frolov backed out of the fight citing lingering injuries.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/cb-dollaway-wederom-zonder-tegenstander-na-blessure-artem-frolov/|title=CB Dollaway again without an opponent after injury Artem Frolov|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-09-07|access-date=2018-09-07|language=nl}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer Khalid Murtazaliev.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/khalid-murtazaliev-vervangt-artem-frolov-tijdens-ufc-moskou/|title=Khalid Murtazaliev replaces Artem Frolov at UFC: Moscow|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Lennart Van Arendonk|date=2018-09-08|access-date=2018-09-09|language=nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Jin Soo Son and [[Mairbek Taisumov]] both missed the required weight for their respective fights. Son weighed in at 137 pounds, 1 pound over the bantamweight non-title fight limit of 136. Meanwhile, Taisumov weighed in at 161 pounds, 5 pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit of 156. Son was fined 20 percent of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Petr Yan]], while Taisumov surrendered 40 percent of his purse to [[Desmond Green]].{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/ufc-fight-night-136-moscow-russia-mark-hunt-aleksei-oleinik-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC Fight Night 136 weigh-in results: Two miss, but headliners set|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-09-14|access-date=2018-09-14}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (UFC Fight Pass)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Aleksei Oleinik]]\n|def.\n|[[Mark Hunt]]\n|Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|1\n|4:26\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Jan Błachowicz]]\n|def.\n|[[Nikita Krylov]]\n|Submission (arm-triangle choke)\n|2\n|2:41\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Shamil Abdurakhimov]]\n|def.\n|[[Andrei Arlovski]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Alexey Kunchenko]]\n|def.\n|[[Thiago Alves (fighter)|Thiago Alves]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 29–28, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (UFC Fight Pass)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|Khalid Murtazaliev\n|def.\n|[[C. Due to a nasal issue that required surgery, the interim champion [[Colby Covington]] was not available for this event. He was also expected to be stripped of his title as soon as the Woodley/Till bout took place.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/7/24/17610216/tyron-woodley-vs-darren-till-slated-for-ufc-228|title=Tyron Woodley vs. Darren Till slated for UFC 228|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun replacement for the bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24341627/kamaru-usman-tagged-backup-tyron-woodley-darrentill-title-fight-ufc-228|title=Kamaru Usman tagged as backup for Tyron Woodley-Darren Till title fight|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2018-08-10|access-date=2018-08-16}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] bout between then champion [[Nicco Montaño]] and former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] title challenger [[Valentina Shevchenko]] was expected to take place at the event, as the co-headliner.{{Cite news|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24130441/nicco-montano-defend-flyweight-title-vs-valentina-shevchenko-ufc-228|title=Nicco Montaño to defend flyweight title vs. Valentina Shevchenko at UFC 228|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2018-07-18|access-date=2018-07-18}} However, before the weigh-ins, Montaño was transported to a hospital due to the effects of weight cutting and the title bout with Shevchenko was cancelled.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/nicco-montano-out-ufc-228-flyweight-title-fight-valentina-shevchenko-dallas|title=Nicco Montano out of UFC 228 flyweight title fight vs. Valentina Shevchenko|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-09-07|access-date=2018-09-07}} Later that day, UFC president [[Dana White]] announced that Montaño was held on October 27, 2018 at [[Avenir Centre]] in [[Moncton, New Brunswick]], Canada.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/06/ufc-debut-event-oct-27-smg-moncton-new-brunswick|title=UFC announces New Brunswick debut for Oct. 27|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-06-07|access-date=2018-07-19}}{{Cite Release|date=2018-06-07|access-date=2018-08-01}}\n\n== Background ==\nWhile the UFC has hosted many events across Canada, the event marked the promotion's first visit to [[New Brunswick]].\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Volkan Oezdemir]] and [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/08/volkan-oezdemir-vs-anthony-smith-bookd-ufc-moncton|title=Volkan Oezdemir vs. Anthony Smith to headline UFC Fight Night 138 in However, it was reported that Tukhugov was removed from the fight due his role in the [[UFC 229#Nurmagomedov–McGregor post-fight controversy|UFC 229 post-fight melee]].{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/report-zubaira-tukhugov-speaks-out-after-slapping-conor-mcgregor-as-promised|title=Zubaira Tukhugov speaks out after slapping Conor McGregor 'as promised'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-07|access-date=2018-10-08}} He was replaced by [[Michael Johnson (fighter)|Michael Johnson]].{{Cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Artem-Lobov-To-Face-Michael-Johnson-At-UFC-Moncton-143885|title=Artem Lobov to face Michael Johnson at UFC Moncton|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Nathan Zur|date=2018-10-15|access-date=2018-10-15}}\n\n[[Gavin Tucker]] was scheduled to face [[Andre Soukhamthath]] at the event. However, Tucker pulled out of the fight in early October citing an undisclosed injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Jonathan Martinez]].{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/jonathan-martinez-vervangt-gavin-tucker-tegen-andre-soukhamthath-tijdens-ufc-moncton/|title=Jonathan Martinez replaces Gavin Tucker against Andre Soukhamthath during UFC Moncton|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-10-10|access-date=2018-10-10|language=nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Johnson weighed in at 147 lb, 1 pound over the featherweight limit of 146 lb. He was fined 20% of his fight purse and his bout against Lobov proceeded at catchweight.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/ufc-fight-night-138-moncton-volkan-oezdemir-anthony-smith-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC Fight Night 138 weigh-in results: Everyone on point except vs. Sijara Eubanks for vacant flyweight title|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-02|access-date=2018-10-02}} Shevchenko was initially expected to face former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] at [[UFC 231]] for the title, but due to UFC 230 being in need of a main event, she was booked against Eubanks sooner.{{Cite news|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/joanna-jedrzejczyk-vs-valentina-shevchenko-set-for-flyweight-title-fight-at-ufc-231/|title=Joanna Jedrzejczyk vs. Valentina Shevchenko set for flyweight title fight at UFC 231|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brandon Wise|date=2018-09-20|access-date=2018-09-21}} Eubanks was expected to face [[Jessica Eye]] at [[UFC 232]], before being moved to this bout.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmaonpoint.com/news/2018/8/31/jessica-eye-vs-sijara-eubanks-targeted-for-ufc-232|title=Jessica Eye vs. Sijara Eubanks Targeted for UFC 232|publisher=mmaonpoint.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-31|access-date=2018-10-04|archive-date=2018-10-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181004225918/https://www.mmaonpoint.com/news/2018/8/31/jessica-eye-vs-sijara-eubanks-targeted-for-ufc-232|url-status=dead}} After the UFC announced Cormier/Lewis on October 9, they confirmed this bout was canceled and that Shevchenko would return to her original bout at [[UFC 231]]. Eubanks remained on the card and faced former title challenger [[Roxanne Modafferi]].{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/sijara-eubanks-will-fight-at-ufc-230-roxanne-modafferi|title=Sijara Eubanks will fight at UFC 230, after all|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-10|access-date=2018-10-10}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former interim [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger (as well as [[Wrestling at the 2000 Summer Olympics|2000 Olympic silver medalist]] and world champion in [[freestyle wrestling]]) [[Yoel Romero]] and [[Paulo Costa]] was scheduled for the event. However, Romero indicated in mid-August that while he has been cleared to fight, his doctors have recommended that he wait another four to five months (early 2019) to allow facial injuries incurred during his most recent fight to fully heal.{{cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24429143/yoel-romero-says-paulo-costa-fight-ufc-230-not-happening-due-doctor-recommendation|title=Yoel Romero: Doctor says I need 4–5 months to heal before next fight|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2018-08-20|access-date=2018-08-21}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Dustin Poirier]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger and [[The Ultimate Fighter 5]] lightweight winner [[Nate Diaz]] was expected to take place at the event. However, on October 10, it was reported that Poirier pulled out due to injury and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/dustin-poirier-injured-out-of-ufc-230-fight-vs-nate-diaz|title=Dustin Poirier injured, out of UFC 230; Dana White says Nate Diaz off card, too|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-09|access-date=2018-10-09}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Brian Kelleher]] and [[Domingo Pilarte (fighter)|Domingo Pilarte]] was expected to take place at the event. However, on October 10, it was reported that Pilarte pulled out due to injury and was replaced by [[Montel Jackson]].{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/montel-jackson-vervangt-domingo-pilarte-tijdens-ufc-230-tegen-brian-kelleher/|title=Montel Jackson replaces Domingo Pilarte during UFC 230 against Brian Kelleher|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-10-10|access-date=2018-10-18|language=nl}}\n\n[[Sultan Aliev]] was expected to face former [[List of Bellator champions#Welterweight Championship|Bellator Welterweight Champion]] [[Lyman Good]] at the event, but he pulled out due to injury. He was replaced by [[Ben Saunders (fighter)|Ben Saunders]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.newsday.com/amp/sports/mixed-martial-arts/ben-saunders-steps-in-to-face-lyman-good-at-ufc-230-1.22130489|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181020053129/https://www.newsday.com/amp/sports/mixed-martial-arts/ben-saunders-steps-in-to-face-lyman-good-at-ufc-230-1.22130489|url-status=dead|archive-date=October 20, 2018|title=Ben Saunders steps in to face Lyman Good at UFC 230|publisher=newsday.com|author=Mark La Monica|date=2018-10-19|access-date=2018-10-19}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champions]] [[Luke Rockhold]] (also former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce Middleweight Champion]]) and [[Chris Weidman]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2018/9/9/17836438/chris-weidman-vs-luke-rockhold-2-official-for-ufc-230|title=Chris Weidman vs. Luke Rockhold 2 official for UFC 230|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander Lee|date=2018-09-09|access-date=2018-10-19}} The two previously met at [[UFC 194]] where Rockhold defeated then champion Weidman with a fourth-round knockout.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/12/13/10021662/ufc-194-results-luke-rockhold-stuns-chris-weidman-to-win-middleweight|title=UFC 194 results: Luke Rockhold stuns Chris Weidman to win middleweight title|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2015-12-13|access-date=2018-10-19}} However, Rockhold pulled out on October 19 due to multiple injuries.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmajunkie.com/2018/10/luke-rockhold-injury-out-ufc-230-chris-weidman/amp|title=Luke Rockhold out of UFC 230 co-headliner vs. Chris Weidman|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-19|access-date=2018-10-19}} He was replaced by another former Strikeforce champion [[Ronaldo Souza]], who was initially scheduled to face former [[WSOF Middleweight Championship|WSOF Middleweight]] and [[WSOF Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[David Branch (fighter)|David Branch]] in the event. Branch instead faced [[Jared Cannonier]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmanews.com/chris-weidman-vs-jacare-souza/amp/|title=Chris Weidman vs. Jacare Souza booked for UFC 230|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Fernando Ouiles Jr.|date=2018-10-19|access-date=2018-10-19}}\n\n[[Ruslan Magomedov]] was expected to face [[Marcos Rogério de Lima]] at the event. However, on October 24, it was reported that he pulled out of the event due to visa issues and was replaced by [[Adam Wieczorek]].{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/adam-wieczorek-replaces-ruslan-magomedov-faces-marcos-rogerio-de-lima-at-ufc-230|title=Adam Wieczorek replaces Ruslan Magomedov, faces Marcos Rogerio de Lima at UFC 230|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2018-10-24|access-date=2018-10-25}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Eubanks and Kelleher both missed the required weight for their respective fights. Eubanks weighed in at 127.2 pounds, 1.2 pound over the flyweight non-title fight limit of 126. Meanwhile, Kelleher weighed in at 137 pounds, 1 pound over the bantamweight non-title fight limit of 136. Both of them were fined 20 percent of their purse, which went to their opponents [[Roxanne Modafferi]] and [[Montel Jackson]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmaweekly.com/ufc-230-weigh-in-results-daniel-cormier-vs-derrick-lewis|title=UFC 230 heavyweight title fight set after Derrick Lewis ekes under the limit|publisher=mmaweekly.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-02|access-date=2018-11-02}} In turn, Kelleher was pulled due to illness on the day of the event.{{Cite Night: Magny vs. Ponzinibbio]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: The Korean Zombie vs. Rodríguez''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 139''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on November 10, 2018, at [[Pepsi Center]] in [[Denver, Colorado]], United States.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/ufc-announces-2018-schedule-september-through-end-of-year|title=UFC announces rest of 2018 schedule from September through end of year|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-07-08|access-date=2018-07-09}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe UFC celebrated its 25th anniversary at this event, given its [[UFC 1|first event]] took place on November 12, 1993, in Denver at the now-demolished [[McNichols Sports Arena]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.denverpost.com/2018/07/07/ufc-25-anniversary-fight-denver|title=UFC announces official 25th anniversary Fight Night was in Denver|publisher=denverpost.com|author=Sara Grant|date=2018-07-07|access-date=2018-07-17}} UFC officials went to a throwback theme for the event, using the original UFC logo, and non-Fox related television graphics were based on the original pay-per-view broadcast.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/ufc-fight-night-139-retro-broadcast-25th-anniversary-denver|title=UFC Fight Night 139 broadcast goes retro for 25th anniversary|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Blue Corner|date=2018-11-10|access-date=2018-11-14}}\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Frankie Edgar]] and former [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[The Korean Zombie|Chan Sung Jung]] was expected to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24153056/frankie-edgar-chan-sung-jung-bout-set-ufc-fight-night-nov-10|title=Frankie Edgar-Chan Sung Jung bout is set for UFC Fight Night on Nov. 10|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date= 2018-07-20|access-date=2018-07-20}} However, on October 26, it was reported that Edgar pulled out due to injury and was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/10/26/18031464/frankie-edgar-out-of-ufc-denver-main-event-yair-rodriguez-steps-in-against-chan-sung-jung|title=Frankie Edgar out of UFC Denver main event, Yair Rodriguez steps in against Chan Sung Jung|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2018-10-26|access-date=2018-10-26}}\n\n[[Ricardo Ramos (fighter)|Ricardo Ramos]] was expected to face [[Ricky Simón]] at the event. However, on October 15, it was reported that Ramos pulled out due to a hand injury, and in turn, Simon was removed from the card as well.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/10/16/17983738/ricardo-ramos-injured-out-ufc-denver-fight-with-ricky-simon|title=Ricardo Ramos injured, out of UFC Denver fight with Ricky Simon|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-10-16|access-date=2018-10-16}}{{Cite news|url=https://www.denverpost.com/2018/10/23/ufc-denver-card-changes-announced/|title=UFC Denver 25th anniversary card changes announced|publisher=denverpost.com|author=Joe Nguyen|date=2018-10-23|access-date=2018-10-24}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Beneil Dariush]] and [[Chris Gruetzemacher]] was expected to take place at the event. However, on October 19, it was reported Gruetzemacher withdrew from the event due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Thiago Moisés]].{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/thiago-moises-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-beneil-dariush-tijdens-ufc-denver5/|title=Thiago Moisés takes a short notice match against Beneil Dariush at UFC Denver|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-10-19|access-date=2018-10-19|language=nl}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Maycee Barber]] and Maia Stevenson was expected to take place at the event. However, on October 20, it was reported that Stevenson pulled out from the event due to injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Hannah Cifers]].{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/hannah-cifers-vervangt-maia-stevenson-tegen-maycee-barber-tijdens-ufc-denver/|title=Hannah Cifers replaces Maia Stevenson against Maycee Barber at UFC Denver|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-10-20|access-date=2018-10-21|language=nl}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Alonzo Menifield]] and [[Saparbek Safarov]] was expected to take place at the event. However it was reported on October 21 that Safarov pulled out from the event for undisclosed reasons and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/alonzo-menifield-vs-saparbek-safarov-gecanceld-voor-ufc-denver/|title=Alonzo Menifield vs. Saparbek Safarov canceled for UFC Denver|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-10-21|language=nl|access-date=2018-10-22}}\n\n[[Jordan Espinosa]] was expected to face [[Mark De La Rosa]] at the event. However, on November 4, it was reported that Espinosa pulled out from the event and was replaced by Joby Sanchez.{{Cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/joby-sanchez-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-mark-de-la-rosa-in-denver/|title=Joby Sanchez grabs short notice party against Mark de la Rosa in Denver|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2018-11-04|author=Marcel Dorff|access-date=2018-11-05|language=nl}}\n\nA bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challengers [[Ray Borg]] and [[Joseph Benavidez]] was expected to take place at the event. However, Borg pulled out on November 7 due to medical issues and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2018/11/7/18072454/ray-borg-ruled-out-of-fight-against-joseph-benavidez-at-ufc-denver|title=Ray Borg ruled out of fight against Joseph Benavidez at UFC Denver|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2018-11-07|access-date=2018-11-07}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Raquel Pennington]] weighed in at 138 lb, 2 pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit of 136 lb. She was fined 20 percent of her purse, which went to her opponent former [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]] [[Germaine de Randamie]] and the bout proceeded at catchweight.{{Cite news|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/platform/amp/2018/11/9/18078442/video-ufc-denver-korean-zombie-vs-yair-rodriguez-official-early-weigh-ins-live-stream|title=UFC Denver weigh-in results and video: Korean Zombie 146, Rodriguez 145.5|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Anton Tabuena|date=2018-11-09|access-date=2018-11-09}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Yair Rodríguez]]\n|def.\n|[[The Korean Zombie|Chan Sung Jung]]\n|KO (elbow)\n|5\n|4:59\n|Tied for latest finish in UFC history.\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Donald Cerrone]]\n|def.\n|[[Mike Perry (fighter)|Mike Perry]]\n|Submission (armbar)\n|1\n|4:46\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (138 lb)\n|[[Germaine de Randamie]]\n|def.\n|[[Raquel Pennington]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Beneil Dariush]]\n|def.\n|[[Thiago Moisés]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–25, 30–25, 30–26)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Maycee Barber]]\n|def.\n|[[Hannah Cifers]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|2\n|2:01\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Michael Trizano]]\n|def.\n|[[Luis Peña (fighter)|Luis Peña]]\n|Decision (split) (28–29, 29–28, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (Fox Sports 1)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Ashley Yoder]]\n|def.\n|[[Amanda Brundage|Amanda Cooper]]\n|Decision (split) (29–28, 27–30, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Bobby Moffett]]\n|vs.\n|[[Chas Skelly]]\n|No Contest (overturned)\n|2\n|2:43\n|Originally a technical submission (brabo choke) win for Moffett; Arena]]\n|city=[[Beijing]], China\n|attendance=10,302{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/ufc-fight-night-141-draws-reported-10302-attendance-in-beijing|title=UFC Fight Night 141 draws Ponzinibbio]]\n|followingevent=[[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters#The Ultimate Fighter 28 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters Finale]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Blaydes vs. Ngannou 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 141''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on November 24, 2018 at [[Cadillac Arena]] in [[Beijing]], China.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/8/5/17652994/ufc-to-debut-in-beijing-on-nov-24|title=UFC to debut in Beijing on Nov. 25|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2018-08-05|access-date=2018-08-05}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's first visit to Beijing. The UFC previously visited [[Mainland China]] for [[UFC Fight Night: Bisping vs. Gastelum]] in November 2017.\n\nA heavyweight rematch between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Francis Ngannou]] and [[Curtis Blaydes]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24393887/beijing|title=UFC heavyweights Francis Ngannou, Curtis Blaydes to meet in Beijing|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2018-08-16|access-date=2018-08-16}} The pairing met previously in April 2016 at [[UFC Fight Night: Rothwell vs. dos Santos]], with Ngannou winning the fight via TKO stoppage at the conclusion of the second round.{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-francis-ngannou-beats-curtis-blaydes-after-nasty-eye-injury-stops-the-fight-041016|title=Francis Ngannou beats Curtis Blaydes after nasty eye injury stops the fight|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-04-10|access-date=2018-08-16}}\n\n[[Shana Dobson]] was scheduled to face [[Wu Yanan (fighter)|Wu Yanan]] at the event. However, Dobson pulled out of the fight in mid-October citing an injury and was replaced by [[Lauren Mueller]].{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/lauren-mueller-replaces-shana-dobson-vs-wu-yanan-ufc-fight-night-141|title=Lauren Mueller replaces Shana Dobson vs. Wu Yanan at UFC Fight Night 141|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-18|access-date=2018-10-18}}\n\n[[Elizeu Zaleski dos Santos]] was expected to face [[Li Jingliang]] at the event. However, Santos pulled out from the bout on October 27 due to a knee injury.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/10/27/18031786/knee-injury-forces-elizeu-zaleski-out-ufc-beijing-li-jingliang|title=Knee injury forces Elizeu Zaleski out of UFC Beijing|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-10-27|access-date=2018-10-27}} He was replaced by [[David Zawada]].{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/david-zawada-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-li-jingliang-tijdens-ufc-beijing/|title=David Zawada tackles short notice party against Li Jingliang during UFC Beijing|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-11-01|access-date=2018-11-01|language=nl}}\n\n[[Frankie Saenz]] was expected to face [[Song Yadong]] at the event. However, on November 7, it was reported that he pulled out of the bout due to injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Vince Morales]].{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/chinese-team-alpha-male-vechters-maken-opwachting-tijdens-ufc-beijing/|title=Chinese Team Alpha Male fighters make an appearance at UFC Beijing|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-11-07|access-date=2018-11-07|language=nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Rashad Coulter]] weighed in at 208 pounds, 2 pounds over the light heavyweight non-title fight limit of 206. Coulter was fined 20 percent of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Hu Yaozong]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc/ufc-beijing-weigh-in-results-rashad-coulter-comes-in-heavy/|title=UFC Beijing weigh in results: Rashad Coulter comes in heavy|date=2018-11-23|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Drake Riggs|access-date=2018-11-23}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (UFC Fight Pass)}}\n{{MMAevent Night 142''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on December 2, 2018 at [[Adelaide Entertainment Centre]] in [[Adelaide]], Australia.{{cite news|url=https://www.news.com.au/sport/boxing/ufc-announces-return-to-adelaide-for-fight-night-event-on-december-2/news-story/b32e27eb432cc8f270a37930b82f04a7|title=UFC announces return to Adelaide for fight night event on December 2|author=Ben Harvy|date=2018-07-08|access-date=2018-07-08|publisher=news.com.au}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event was the second that the promotion has contested in Adelaide, following [[UFC Fight Night: Miocic vs. Hunt]] in May 2015.\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Junior dos Santos]] and [[Tai Tuivasa]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24527573/junior-dos-santos-tai-tuivasa-fight-main-event-ufc-card-adelaide-south-australia|title=Junior dos Santos, Tai Tuivasa to headline Dec. 2 card in Australia|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2018-08-30|access-date=2018-08-31}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: United States vs. United Kingdom]] lightweight winner [[Ross Pearson]] was expected to face [[Joseph Duffy (fighter)|Joseph Duffy]] at the event. However, Pearson announced on 7 November that he was out of the bout due to a broken nose and subsequent surgery to correct the injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/ross-pearson-out-duffy-fight/|title=Ross Pearson out of UFC Adelaide fight with Joseph Duffy|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Jon Fuentes|date=2018-11-07|access-date=2018-11-15}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Damir Ismagulov]], but Duffy pulled out of citing a rib injury.{{Cite news|url=https://www.flocombat.com/articles/6270242-joe-duffy-vs-damir-ismagulov-set-for-ufc-adelaide-immediately-canceled|title=Joe Duffy vs. Damir Ismagulov set for UFC Adelaide, immediately vanceled|publisher=flocombat.com|author=Nolan King|date=2018-11-18|access-date=2018-11-18}} Ismagulov then faced fellow newcomer Alex Gorgees.{{Cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/ufc-debutanten-damir-ismagulov-en-alex-gorgees-maken-opwachting-tijdens-ufc-adelaide/|title=UFC debutants Damir Ismagulov and Alex Gorgees make an appearance at UFC Adelaide|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-11-21|access-date=2018-11-21|language=nl}}\n\n[[Ashkan Mokhtarian]] was scheduled to face promotional newcomer [[Kai Kara-France]] at the event. However, on 20 November, Mokhtarian pulled out of the fight citing injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/ashkan-mokhtarian-out-ufc-adelaide-seeks-anzac-opponent-for-kai-kara-france|title=Ashkan Mokhtarian out of UFC Adelaide, UFC seeks Anzac opponent for Kai Kara-France|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2018-11-21|access-date=2018-11-21}} He was replaced by Elias Garcia.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/elias-garcia-in-vs-kai-kara-france-ufc-fight-night-142-adelaide|title=Elias Garcia steps in vs. Kai Kara-France at UFC Fight Night 142 in Adelaide|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-23|access-date=2018-11-26}}\n\n[[Luke Jumeau]] was scheduled to face [[Geoff Neal]] at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.stuff.co.nz/sport/combat-sports/107965152/kiwi-luke-jumeau-books-ufc-return-against-geoff-neal-in-adelaide.html|title=Kiwi Luke Jumeau books UFC return against Geoff Neal in Adelaide|website=Stuff|language=en|access-date=2018-10-20}} However, it was reported on 9 November 2018 that Jumeau pulled from the card due to injury, resulting cancellation of the bout by the UFC official.{{Cite web|url=http://www.fightnewsaustralia.com/ufc-adelaide-luke-jumeau-injury-forces-him-off-card/|title=UFC Adelaide: Injury forces Luke Jumeau off card – Fight News Australia|language=en|access-date=2018-12-10}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Junior dos Santos]] \n|def.\n|[[Tai
