Ortega'|date=2018-12-21|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Staff|access-date=2018-12-21}}}}\n\n'''''UFC 231: Holloway vs. Ortega''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on December 8, 2018 at [[Scotiabank Arena]] in [[Toronto]], [[Ontario]], Canada.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/ufc-announces-2018-schedule-september-through-end-of-year|title=UFC announces rest of 2018 schedule from September through end of year|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-07-08|access-date=2018-07-09}}\n\n== Background ==\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] bout between [[Max Holloway]] and [[Brian Ortega]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/max-holloway-vs-brian-ortega-official-ufc-231|title=Max Holloway vs. Brian Ortega title fight officially back on at UFC 231 in Toronto|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-01|access-date=2018-10-01}} The fight was originally scheduled to take place at [[UFC 226]], but Holloway pulled out due to \"concussion like symptoms\".{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/max-holloway-withdraws-ufc-226-concussion-like-symptom|title=Max Holloway out of UFC 226, hospitalized with concussion-like symptoms|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2018-07-04|access-date=2018-07-04}}\n\nIn the co-headlining slot, a bout for the vacant [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Valentina Shevchenko]] and former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] took place. If victorious, Jędrzejczyk would become the first woman to win titles in different divisions.{{Cite news|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/joanna-jedrzejczyk-vs-valentina-shevchenko-set-for-flyweight-title-fight-at-ufc-231/|title=Joanna Jedrzejczyk vs. Valentina Shevchenko set for flyweight title fight at UFC 231|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brandon Wise|date=2018-09-20|access-date=2018-09-21}} They met previously in amateur muay thai and kickboxing, with Shevchenko winning all three encounters.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2016/07/valentina-shevchenko-has-3-wins-over-ufc-champ-joanna-jedrzejczyk-whos-now-a-supporter|title=Valentina Shevchenko has 3 wins over UFC champ Joanna Jedrzejczyk, who's now a supporter|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Justin Park|date=2016-07-23|access-date=2018-09-22}} On October 2, the UFC announced that Shevchenko would face [[Sijara Eubanks]] at [[UFC 230]] instead, as that event was in need of a headliner.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/ufc-230-main-event-valentina-shevchenko-vs-sijara-eubanks-vacant-flyweight-title-joanna-jedrzejczyk|title=UFC 230 finally has main event: Valentina Shevchenko vs. Sijara Eubanks for vacant flyweight title|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-10-02|access-date=2018-10-02}} The fight between Shevchenko and Jędrzejczyk was put back together a week later though, as a fight between then [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight]] and [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Daniel Cormier]] and [[Derrick Lewis]] wound up filling the headlining slot at [[UFC 230]].{{Cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/24943833/daniel-cormier-derrick-lewis-headline-ufc-230|title=Daniel Cormier-Derrick Lewis official; Valentina Shevchenko moved back to UFC 231|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2018-10-09|access-date=2018-10-09}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Gilbert Burns]] and [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] was previously scheduled to take place in February 2018 at [[UFC on Fox: Emmett vs. Stephens]]. However, Burns was pulled from the pairing during the week leading up to the event after medical officials deemed that it was unsafe for Burns to cut the necessary weight for the 156-pound limit.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/2/21/17036504/gilbert-burns-vs-olivier-aubin-mercier-scratched-from-ufc-on-fox-28|title=Gilbert Burns vs. Olivier Aubin-Mercier scratched from UFC on FOX 28|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2018-02-21|access-date=2018-02-21}} The fight has been rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/poging-2-gilbert-burns-vs-olivier-aubin-mercier-tijdens-ufc-231-in-toronto/|title=Attempt 2: Gilbert Burns vs. Olivier Aubin-Mercier at UFC 231 in Toronto|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-08-22|access-date=2018-08-22|language=nl}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3]] heavyweight winner [[Antônio Carlos Júnior]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter Nations: Canada vs. Australia|The Ultimate Fighter: Nations]] middleweight winner [[Elias Theodorou]] was initially scheduled to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Santos vs. Anders]]. However, Júnior pulled out on August 28 due to injury.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/8/28/17793374/antonio-carlos-junior-out-of-ufc-sao-paulo-fight-against-elias-theodorou|title=Antonio Carlos Junior out of UFC Sao Paulo fight against Elias Theodorou|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2018-08-27|access-date=2018-08-28}} The pairing was then rescheduled for this event.{{cite Holloway vs. Ortega]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 232|UFC 232: Jones vs. Gustafsson 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on Fox: Lee vs. Iaquinta 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 31''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on December 15, 2018, at [[Fiserv Forum]] in [[Milwaukee]], [[Wisconsin]], United States.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/ufc-announces-2018-schedule-september-through-end-of-year|title=UFC announces rest of 2018 schedule from September through end of year|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-07-08|access-date=2018-07-09}}{{Cite news|url=https://www.jsonline.com/story/sports/2018/07/07/new-arena-new-arena-host-ufc-dec-15-host-ufc-dec-15/764182002/|title=UFC is coming back to Milwaukee, this time to new arena|publisher=jsonline.com|author=Ben Steele|date= 2018-07-07|access-date=2018-07-19}}\n\n== Background ==\nAfter previously contesting two events ([[UFC Live: Hardy vs. Lytle]] and [[UFC 164]]) in Milwaukee at [[Bradley Center]], the event was the first for the promotion at the newly built venue.\n\nThe event also marked the end of the [[Fox UFC|UFC's seven years with Fox]] as a new broadcast deal with [[ESPN]] was slated to begin in January 2019.{{Cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/23581729/espn-ufc-reach-five-year-television-rights-deal|title=ESPN to broadcast 30 UFC events per year during 5-year deal|publisher=espn.com|author=ESPN Newswire|date=2018-05-23|access-date=2018-09-05}} It was also the last UFC event to air on network television until [[UFC on ABC: Holloway vs. Kattar]] in January 2021.\n\nA lightweight rematch between former interim title challenger [[Kevin Lee (fighter)|Kevin Lee]] and [[Al Iaquinta]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Kevin-LeeAl-Iaquinta-Rematch-to-Serve-as-Main-Event-for-UFC-on-Fox-31-in-Milwaukee-143713l|title=Kevin Lee-Al Iaquinta rematch to serve as main event for UFC on Fox 31 in Milwaukee|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2018-10-10|access-date=2018-10-11}} The pairing met previously in February 2014 at [[UFC 169]] with Iaquinta winning the fight via unanimous decision.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2014/2/1/5369544/ufc-169-results-al-iaquinta-wins-entertaining-scrap-against-kevin-lee|title=UFC 169 results: Al Iaquinta wins entertaining scrap against Kevin Lee|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2014-02-01|access-date=2018-10-11}}\n\n[[Erik Koch]] was expected to face promotional newcomer [[Dwight Grant]] in a welterweight bout. However, Koch was removed from the card on November 28 for undisclosed reasons and replaced by [[Zak Ottow]].{{Cite news|url=https://themmatakeover.com/zak-ottow-faces-dwight-grant/|title=Erik Koch out; Zak Ottow now faces Dwight Grant|publisher=themmatakeover.com|author=Bryant Jenkins|date=2018-11-28|access-date=2018-11-29|archive-date=2018-11-29|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181129184124/https://themmatakeover.com/zak-ottow-faces-dwight-grant/|url-status=dead}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Jessica-Rose Clark]] and [[Andrea Lee (fighter)|Andrea Lee]] was expected to take place at the event. However, Clark was forced out of the bout on weigh-in day as she was hospitalized due to a weight cutting issue and deemed medically unfit to compete by UFC physicians. As a result, the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/jessica-rose-clark-vs-andrea-lee-scrapped-from-ufc-on-fox-31/|title=Jessica-Rose Clark vs. Andrea Lee Pulled From UFC on surgery due to an abdominal hernia of the intestine and a twisted and collapsed bowel.{{cite web |last1=Pishna |first1=Ken |title=Robert Whittaker Emerges from Emergency Dual Surgery |url=https://www.mmaweekly.com/robert-whittaker-emerges-from-emergency-dual-surgery |website=MMAWeekly.com |access-date=3 May 2019}} The pairing eventually met 2 years later at [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Gastelum]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-news-robert-whittaker-vs-kelvin-gastelum-with-paulo-costa-out-april-17|title=Robert Whittaker gets Kelvin Gastelum as replacement for UFC main event on April 17|author=John Morgan, Marc Bohn and Nolan King|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|date=2021-03-16|access-date=April 3, 2021}} The cast was announced on July 11, 2018.\n\n==Cast==\n===Coaches===\n{{Col-begin}}\n{{Col-break}}\n{{color box|#0070FF|border=silver}} '''Team Whittaker''':\n* [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]], Head Coach\n* Fabricio [[Macy Chiasson]], [[Pannie Kianzad]], [[Bea Malecki]], and Marciea Allen.\n\n==Episodes==\n'''Episode 1: Biggest and Baddest (August 29, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/08/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-1-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 1 recap|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-30|access-date=2018-08-30}}\n*The fighters arrived in Las Vegas to begin the training and evaluations prior to the teams being picked.\n*Gastelum wins the coin toss and chose to pick the first fight. That means Whittaker would have the first fighter pick and the fighters were picked in the following order:\n\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n!Coach\n!1st Pick\n!2nd Pick\n!3rd Pick\n!4th Pick\n!5th Pick\n!6th Pick\n!7th Pick\n!8th Pick\n|-\n!Whittaker\n|Anderson da Silva (HW)\n|Julija Stoliarenko (WFW)\n|Juan Francisco Espino Dieppa (HW)\n|Leah Letson (WFW)\n|Michel Batista (HW)\n|Larissa Pacheco (WFW)\n|Przemysław Mysiala (HW)\n|Katharina Lehner (WFW)\n|-\n!Gastelum\n|Ben Sosoli (HW)\n|Macy Chiasson (WFW)\n|Maurice Greene (HW)\n|Pannie Kianzad (WFW)\n|Josh Parisian (HW)\n|Bea Malecki (WFW)\n|Justin Frazier (HW)\n|Marciea Allen (WFW)\n|}\n* With control of fight selection for the first matchup, Gastelum chose his No. 3 pick Maurice Greene to take on Team Whittaker's No. 7 pick Przemyslaw Mysiala in a heavyweight matchup.\n* '''Maurice Greene''' defeated Przemysław Mysiala via KO (punch) in Round 1\n* Team Gastelum retained control of fight selection, and with a women's featherweight bout up next, he selected his No. 4 pick Pannie Kianzad to fight Team Whittaker's No. 8 pick Katharina Lehner.\n\n'''Episode 2: Throw Leather (September 5, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-2-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 2 recap|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-09-05|access-date=2018-09-06}}\n* Gastelum and his assistant coaches worry about Justin Frazier whose [[aerobic exercise|cardio]] isn't up to par with the other fighters during training. He retreated to the bathroom to throw up after practice.\n* Pannie Kianzad breaks her tooth while trying to get her stuck [[headgear]] off. She has to go to the dentist but has major anxiety during the visit and leaves. But she faces her fears on her second visit and undergoes surgery to replace the damaged front tooth with a dental false tooth over the root.\n* '''Pannie Kianzad''' defeated Katharina Lehner via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* Whittaker announced the next men's heavyweight fight: Justin Frazier vs. Anderson da Silva\n\n'''Episode 3: War Tanks (September 12, 2018)'''\n*After two weeks training with Team Gastelum, Justin Frazier does a 180 and turns himself around. He gets a mental and physical boost; no more fatigue and cramping.\n*While at the ''TUF'' house, the Team Gastelum fighters have trouble communicating with Team Whittaker fighters since the majority are from different countries. To help matters, Team Whittaker's Juan Francisco Espino Dieppa tries his best as his team's [[interpreter]]. But being the lone American on Team Whittaker, Leah Letson feels left out by the language barrier.\n*Maurice Greene decides to create some tension between the teams and starts impersonating Michel Batista because he believes Batista is a \"snake in the grass\", pretending not to understand English to get the upper hand.\n*'''Justin Frazier''' defeated Anderson da Silva via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n*Whittaker announced the women's featherweight fight: Julija Stoliarenko vs. Marciea Allen.\n\n'''Episode 4: A Win is a Win (September 19, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-4-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 4 recap|date=2018-09-20|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-10-25|language=en-US}}\n* Julija Stoliarenko, the [[kempo]] [[karate]] champion, discussed the honor of being the first female Lithuanian fighter to represent her country to appear on a UFC.\n* Gastelum brought in Brian Beaumont, who was one of Gastelum's first MMA coaches, as a special coach to assist his team. During one of the demo sessions, Beaumont surprised Gastelum and awarded him his black belt in [[Brazilian jiu-jitsu]].\n*In order to change the setting from the ''TUF'' house, Whittaker takes his team to Lake Spring Mountain to workout and have a [[paddleboard]] race.\n*Leah Letson continued to struggle with the culture of Team Whittker as she thought the training sessions were not challenging enough for her.\n*'''Julija Stoliarenko''' defeated Marciea Allen in by submission (arm bar) in Round 1.\n*Gastelum selected Josh Parisian and Whittaker picked Michel Batista for the third heavyweight quarterfinal.\n\n'''Episode 5: Life is a Fight (September 26, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/09/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-5-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 5 recap|date=2018-09-27|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-10-25|language=en-US}}\n* Following fight between Juliija Stoliarenko and Marciea Allen, Allen lamented her loss while Stoliarenko celebrated her win with alcohol by the pool and ended up throwing up much of what she had consumed.\n* Meanwhile, Michael Batista, who won bronze in wrestling at [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics#Medal summary|2008 Summer Olympics]], prepared his training for the bout against Josh Parisian who quit his job as a quality investigator for an IT company after he got selected as a contestant for this event.\n* John Walker, an [[NFL]] veteran and Gastelum's [[strength and conditioning coach]], was invited to ''TUF'' house as a motivation speaker for the fighters.\n* Leah Letson, who felt communication disconnect with her non-English speaking teammates and Whittaker's hand off style, mentioned the idea to switch to Gastelum team.\n*'''Michel Batista''' defeated Josh Parisian via TKO (punches) in Round 2.\n* The third women's featherweight quarterfinal was set between Bea Malecki and Leah Letson.\n\n'''Episode 6: Comfortable with the Uncomfortable (October 3, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-6-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 6 recap|date=2018-10-04|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-10-25|language=en-US}}\n* Leah Letson detailed how she had signed by UFC to fight against [[Yana Kunitskaya]] and landed the spot in TUF 28 after Kunitskaya pulled from the bout to meet [[Cris Cyborg]] at [[UFC 222]], and expressed her satisfaction with her training with Team Whittaker after attention was given to her training intensity.\n* Bea Malecki, a formal Air National Guard and Swedish and European Thai boxing champion, did not wanted to brand as the \"pretty girl who can not fight\" and planned to keep the fight against Letson on the feet to avoid her shortcoming on the ground game.\n* Back at the ''TUF'' gym, Gastelum brought [[Uriah Hall]] as a special guest coach to assist with the fighters training session.\n* '''Leah Letson''' defeated Bea Malecki via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n* Whittaker picks Juan Francisco Espino Dieppa to meet Team Gastelum's Ben Sosoli in the fourth and final heavyweight quarterfinal.\n\n'''Episode 7: I never have Fear (October 24, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-7-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 7 recap|date=2018-10-25|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-10-25|language=en-US}}\n* Leah Letson showed a pair of black eyes and swollen face from her hard-fought win over Bea Malechi and back on board with Team Whittaker after received help and guidance from the coaches.\n* Back in the ''TUF'' house, tension rose after Maurice Greene accused Espino Dieppa being a \"fake person\". Issues were resolved after Greene admitted he was being emotional and he hashed out the problem with Espino Dieppa with civil communication.\n* '''Juan Francisco Espino Dieppa''' defeated Ben Sosoli via unanimous decision in two rounds.\n* Whittaker announced the next women's featherweight fight: Larissa Pacheco vs. Macy Chiasson.\n'''Episode 8: Be First to be Last (October 31, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/10/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-8-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 8 recap|date=2018-11-01|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-11-02|language=en-US}}\n*Macy Chiasson who was preparing with her fight against Larissa Pacheco was growing uneasy with Maurice Greene's constant late night drinking, getting drunk and making noise when the others were sleeping.\n*The drama continues with Maurice Greene when Anderson da Silva started talking about how Maurice would be \"easy money\" if they get to fight, causing Greene to lose his temper. Also, Anderson decides to smack talk to Justin Frazier saying during their fight, Frazier \"hit him like a pussy\".\n*Whittaker believed Pacheco would win the fight as she was more experience and competed in UFC before, while Gastelum paid a high regards on Chiasson's skills even she had only a short MMA professional career.\n* '''Macy Chiasson''' defeated Larissa Pacheco by TKO (punches) in Round 1.\n* [[Dana White]] announced the semifinal match-ups:\n**Michel Batista vs. Justin Frazier\n**Juan Francisco Espino Dieppa vs. Maurice Greene\n**Julija Stoliarenko vs. Pannie Kianzad\n**Macy Chiasson vs. Leah Letson\n\n'''Episode 9: Act Like a Professional (November 7, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-9-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 9 recap|date=2018-11-08|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-11-09|language=en-US}}\n* After the semi-final fight announcements, Maurice Greene has a verbal altercation with Anderson da Silva, continuing their last heated conversation the night before. Gastelum and his coaches sit down with Greene and talk to him about his drinking problem and informed him to behave himself and act professional.\n* After the altercation, Coach Whittaker holds a team dinner at the gym just to get tensions down and his team's spirits back up.\n* Being too overwhelmed by all the drama, Marciea Allen considers leaving the show and even books a flight home.\n* Team Gastelum celebrates Macy Chiasson's birthday at the ''TUF'' house giving her a bottle of ''Don Julio 1942'' and her teammates getting her balloons and a cake. Also team Gastelum each gets a ''TUF 28'' tattoo to commemorate their participation in the event. However Marciea Allen choose not to participate, citing personal problems with Greene's behavior and just wanted to go home.\n* A team meeting is called in the house. Marciea tells Maurice that his behavior makes her uncomfortable and she might want to leave the show because of him. Maurice apologizes to her and expresses he is disappointed and embarrassed on his own conduct in the house. after seeing all her teammates caring about her well-being, Marciea decides to stay.\n* '''Justin Frazier''' defeated Michel Batista via technical knockout in Round One.\n\n'''Episode 10: Made For This (November 14, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-10-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 10 recap|date=2018-11-15|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-11-15|language=en-US}}\n*Still not be able to deal with the pressures of the show, Maurice Greene smokes a cigarette outside the ''TUF'' house. He believes smoking would not affect his performance. However, Justin Frazier confronts Greene about his smoking, and he tears up, bringing up his family's history with [[alcoholism]]. \n*Back at the ''TUF'' gym, Gastelum has been disappointed in Greene's actions, but offers his support. Assistant coach Terrell Hunsinger runs Greene through \"regrouping drills\" by running sprints and other exercises, having Greene spell out this favorite alcoholic beverages for his \"childish behavior\".\n*Dana organizes a trip to have the coaches bring their teams to a local waterpark for a rest day. A competitive Whittaker wins a race on the water slide over Gastelum.\n*'''Pannie Kianzad''' defeated Julija Stoliarenko via unanimous decision.\n\n'''Episode 11: Be loose, be Free, be me (November 21, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-11-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 11 recap|date=2018-11-22|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-11-22|language=en-US}}\n* Some members of Whittaker's team expresses their dissatisfaction with the training intensity and starts some sparring during their grappling training session. Whittaker and his assistant coaches are angry and frustrated with the team's behavior and they give the fighters a speech; if they don't like their training, then get out of the way. Do that's exactly what they do by having their own training session during their free time at the gym.\n* Back at the ''TUF'' house, the fighters watched [[UFC 227]] on [[pay per view|PPV]]. Tensions spark when Marciea fast forwards through promotional clips for [[UFC 229]], which displeases some of the fighters including Juan Espino who wanted to see it. A sober Maurice Greene decides to take control of the situation, resulting in a tense confrontation between the two. And the assistant coaches catching Greene smoking outside, much to their dislike.\n* On the annual Coaches Challenge, a 5 kilometer race between Whittaker and Gastelum under 106 degrees heat, at the [[University of Las Vegas]] track, was set with the winning coach received $10,000 and each winning team member received $1,500 prize. Whittaker won the race easily, and his team took the cash prize.\n* '''Juan Francisco Espino Dieppa''' defeated Maurice Greene via submission (rear naked choke) in Round 1.\n\n'''Episode 12: One Thousand Percent (November 28, 2018)'''{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/the-ultimate-fighter-28-heavy-hitters-episode-12-recap-ufc-tuf|title='The Ultimate Fighter 28: Heavy Hitters' Episode 12 recap|date=2018-11-29|work=MMAjunkie|access-date=2018-11-29|language=en-US}}\n*Team Gastelum's Macy Chiasson prepares for her upcoming fight with Leah Letson. And Gastelum brings in UFC light heavyweight contender [[Ilir Latifi]] as a guest coach to work with Chiasson on grappling techniques, but Chiasson is overwhelmed with learning new moves only two days before her fight and has a mental breakdown.\n*After Chiasson goes through an intense weight cut, Gastelum holds a final team dinner and invites a special guest, UFC flyweight champion [[Henry Cejudo]] for some inspirational advice.\n* '''Macy Chiasson''' defeated Leah Letson via KO (knees to the body) in Round 1.\n\n==Tournament bracket==\n===Heavyweight bracket===\n{{Round8\n\n\n||{{color Finale\n|image=Tuf28Poster.png\n|image_size=225px\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2018|11|30}}\n|venue=[[Palms Casino Resort|Pearl Theatre at Palms Casino Blaydes vs. Ngannou 2]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: dos Santos vs. Tuivasa]]\n}}\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters Finale''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 28 Finale''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on November 30, 2018 at [[Palms Casino Resort|Pearl Theatre at Palms Casino Resort]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]], United States.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/07/ufc-announces-2018-schedule-september-through-end-of-year|title=UFC announces rest of 2018 schedule from September through end of year|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-07-08|access-date=2018-07-09}}\n\n===Background===\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner [[Kamaru Usman]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/ufc-planeja-dos-anjos-x-kamaru-usman-como-luta-principal-do-tuf-28-finale.ghtml|title=UFC plans Dos Anjos vs. Kamaru Usman as TUF 28 Finale Main Fight|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2018-09-12|access-date=2018-09-13|language=pt}}\n\nThe heavyweight and women's featherweight finals of The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters are expected to take place at the event.\n\nFormer [[List of Strikeforce champions|Strikeforce]] and [[List of WEC champions#Lightweight Championship|WEC Lightweight Champion]] (as well as former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger) [[Gilbert Melendez]] was scheduled to face [[Arnold Allen (fighter)|Arnold Allen]] at the event. However, Melendez pulled out of the fight on November 5 citing an injury and was replaced by former [[List of WSOF champions#Featherweight Championship|WSOF Featherweight Champion]] [[Ricky Glenn]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/11/5/18065158/rick-glenn-steps-in-for-injured-gilbert-melendez-fights-arnold-allen-at-tuf-28-finale|title=Rick Glenn steps in for injured Gilbert Melendez, fights Arnold Allen at TUF 28 Finale|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2018-11-05|access-date=2018-11-05}} In turn, Allen pulled out of the fight on November 16 citing a cut he received while training.{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-Arnold-Allen-Injured-Out-of-TUF-28-Finale-Bout-against-Rick-Glenn-145605|title=Arnold Allen injured, out of TUF 28 Finale bout against Rick Glenn|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2018-11-16|access-date=2018-11-16}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Kevin Aguilar (mixed martial artist)|Kevin Aguilar]].{{Cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/lfa-kampioen-kevin-aguilar-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-tegen-rick-glenn-tijdens-de-tuf-28-finale/|title=LFA Champion Kevin Aguilar tackles short notice battle against Rick Glenn during the TUF 28 Final|publisher=mmadna.nl.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-11-17|access-date=2018-11-29}}\n\n[[Ashlee Evans-Smith]] was expected to face promotional newcomer [[Antonina Shevchenko]] at the event. However, Evans-Smith pulled out on November 8 and was replaced by [[Ji Yeon Kim]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/11/8/18076130/antonina-shevchenko-gets-new-opponent-at-tuf-28-finale-with-ashlee-evans-smith-out|title=Antonina Shevchenko gets new opponent at TUF 28 Finale with Ashlee Evans-Smith out|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2018-11-08|access-date=2018-11-08}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between Boston Salmon and [[Khalid Taha]] was initially scheduled for this event.{{Cite news|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/report-boston-salmon-faces-khalid-taha-tuf-28-finale|title=Boston Salmon faces Khalid Taha at TUF 28 Finale|publisher=fightful.com|author=David Tees|date=2018-10-20|access-date=2018-11-29}} However the pairing was removed from the card for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite news|url=https://thebodylockmma.com/ufc/boston-salmon-ive-got-the-best-credentials-in-boxing-in-the-ufc/|title=Boston Salmon: \"I’ve got the best credentials in boxing in the UFC\"|publisher=thebodylockmma.com|author=Michael Fielder|date=2018-11-26|access-date=2018-11-29}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Glenn and Kim both missed the required weight for their respective fights. Glenn weighed in at 148.5 pounds, 2.5 pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit of 146. Meanwhile, Kim weighed in at 130.5 pounds, 4.5 pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit of 126. Both of them were fined 20 percent of their purse, which went to their opponents Aguilar and Shevchenko. The bouts proceeded at catchweight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/ufc-tuf-28-finale-rafael-dos-anjos-kamaru-usman-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=TUF 28 Finale weigh-in results: Headliners set, but two miss|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-29|access-date=2018-11-29}}\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on September 22, 2018 at [[Ginásio do Ibirapuera]] in [[São Paulo]], [[Brazil]].{{cite 22|publisher=sherdog.com|author=João Baptista|date=2018-05-08|access-date=2018-05-08}}\n\n==Background==\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Glover Teixeira]] and [[Jimi Manuwa]] was expected to serve as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/08/glover-teixeira-vs-jimi-manuwa-headlines-ufc-fight-night-137-sao-paulo-brazil|title=Glover Teixeira vs. Jimi Manuwa set to headline UFC Fight Night 137 in Sao Paulo|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-03|access-date=2018-08-03}} However, Teixeira pulled out on August 14 due to a shoulder injury.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/08/injury-knocks-glover-teixeira-from-ufc-fight-night-137-main-event-vs-jimi-manuwa|title=Injury knocks Glover Teixeira from UFC Fight Night 137 main event vs. Jimi Manuwa|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-14|access-date=2018-08-15}} He was replaced by [[Thiago Santos]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmanews.com/jimi-manuwa-vs-thiago-santos-official-for-sao-paulo/|title=Jimi Manuwa vs. Thiago Santos official for São Paulo|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Clyde Aidoo|date=2018-08-21|access-date=2018-08-22}} In turn, Manuwa pulled out on September 16 due to a torn hamstring and was replaced by [[Eryk Anders]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/9/16/17868434/mma-news-jimi-manuwa-injured-eryk-anders-vs-thiago-santos-ufc-sao-paulo-main-event|title=Jimi Manuwa injured, Eryk Anders now fights Thiago Santos in UFC Sao Paulo main event|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2018-09-16|access-date=2018-09-17|archive-date=2018-09-17|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180917105224/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/9/16/17868434/mma-news-jimi-manuwa-injured-eryk-anders-vs-thiago-santos-ufc-sao-paulo-main-event|url-status=dead}}{{Cite Henrique]] at the event. However, Godbeer pulled out of the fight in early August citing injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Ryan Spann]].{{cite news|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/mark-godbeer-sofre-lesao-e-klb-enfrenta-ryan-spann-no-ufc-sao-paulo.ghtml|title=Mark Godbeer suffers injury, and KLB faces Ryan Spann at UFC Sao Paulo|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Camilo Pinheiro Machado, Evelyn Rodrigues and Marcelo Barone|date=2018-08-03|access-date=2018-08-03|language=pt}}\n\n[[Neil Magny]] was scheduled to face [[Alex Oliveira]] at the event. However Magny was removed from the pairing on August 22 in favor of a matchup with [[Santiago Ponzinibbio]] in November at [[UFC Fight Night: Magny vs. Ponzinibbio]].{{cite web|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/neil-magny-deixa-luta-em-sp-e-deve-encarar-ponzinibbio-no-ufc-argentina.ghtml|title=Neil Magny leaves fight in SP to face Ponzinibbio in UFC Argentina|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone and Raphael Marinho|date=2018-08-22|access-date=2018-08-22|language=pt}}{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/8/23/17773182/neil-magny-santiago-ponzinibbio-headlines-ufc-buenos-aires-november|title=Neil Magny vs. Santiago Ponzinibbio headlines UFC Buenos Aires in November|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-08-23|access-date=2018-08-23}} He was replaced by [[Carlo Pedersoli Jr.]]{{Cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Carlo-Pedersoli-Jr-Steps-in-for-Neil-Magny-Faces-Alex-Olivieira-At-UFC-Sao-Paulo-141397|title=Carlo Pedersoli Jr. steps in for Neil Magny, faces Alex Olivieira at UFC São Paulo|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tudor Leonte|date=2018-08-23|access-date=2018-08-23}}\n\n[[Ketlen Vieira]] was expected to face former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] and [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Tonya Evinger]] at the event. However on August 7, Vieira pulled out due to a knee injury.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/8/27/17789270/ketlen-vieira-out-of-ufc-sao-paulo-fight-with-tonya-evinger-will-require-surgery|title=Ketlen Vieira out of UFC Sao Paulo fight with Tonya Evinger, will require surgery|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2018-08-27|access-date=2018-08-28}} Evinger was removed from the card and then rescheduled for a future event against a different opponent.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/08/ufc-229-tonya-evinger-vs-aspen-ladd|title=Tonya Evinger moves to UFC 229, will face undefeated Aspen Ladd|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-08-29|access-date=2018-08-29}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter Nations: Canada vs. Australia|The Ultimate Fighter: Nations]] middleweight winner [[Elias Theodorou]] was expected to face [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3]] heavyweight winner [[Antônio Carlos Júnior]] at the event. (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner) and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/robert-whittaker-vs-kelvin-gastelum-official-for-ufc-234-in-melbourne|title=Robert Whittaker vs. Kelvin Gastelum official and collapsed bowel that forced him to undergo emergency surgery later that day.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmaweekly.com/robert-whittaker-emerges-from-emergency-dual-surgery|title=Robert Whittaker emerges from emergency dual surgery|publisher=mmaweekly.com|author=Ken Pishna|date=2019-02-10|access-date=2019-02-10}} As a result of the last-minute change, the three-round co-main event between [[Israel Adesanya]] and former champion [[Anderson Silva]] was bumped up to the main event spot.{{Cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/ufc/story/_/id/25959907/middleweight-champion-robert-whittaker-ruled-title-defense-fight|title=Middleweight champion Robert Whittaker ruled out for title defense fight|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2019-02-09|access-date=2019-02-09}}\n\n[[Ryan Spann]] was expected to face [[Jimmy Crute]] at the event. However, on January 25, it was reported that Spann was forced to pull out due to a hand injury and was replaced by [[Sam Alvey]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/sam-alvey-in-injured-ryan-spann-jim-crute-ufc-234-melbourne|title=Sam Alvey in for Ryan Spann vs. Jim Crute at UFC 234 in Melbourne|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-25|access-date=2018-11-27}}\n\nAlex Gorgees was expected to face [[Jalin Turner]] at the event. However, it was reported that, on January 27, Gorgees was pulled from the card for an undisclosed reason and he was replaced by [[Callan Potter]].{{Cite on March 2, 2019, at [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]].{{Cite Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-04|access-date=2018-11-04}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between two-time champion [[Jon Jones]] and challenger [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/jon-jones-vs-anthony-smith-booked-ufc-235|title=Jon Jones set to defend title vs. Anthony Smith at UFC 235|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2019-01-06|access-date=2019-01-07}}\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Tyron Woodley]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner [[Kamaru Usman]] served as the co-headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/ufc-235-tyron-woodley-booked-vs-kamaru-usman-not-colby-covington-title-fight|title=Tyron Woodley set to defend title vs. Kamaru Usman – not Colby Covington – at UFC 235|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2019-01-06|access-date=2019-01-07}}\n\nAs a result of the cancellation of [[UFC 233]], a welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Robbie Lawler]] and promotional newcomer and former [[List of Bellator champions#Welterweight Championship|Bellator Welterweight World Champion]] [[Ben Askren]] (also former [[List of ONE Championship champions#Welterweight World Championship|ONE Welterweight Champion]]) was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/12/13/18139529/ben-askren-vs-robbie-lawler-rescheduled-for-ufc-235|title=Ben Askren vs. Robbie Lawler rescheduled for UFC 235|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2018-12-13|access-date=2018-12-13}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and [[Aspen Ladd]] was expected to take place at the event. However, on January 31, it was reported the matchup was scrapped due to undisclosed reasons and both fighters are expected to face new opponents in other events.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/ex-champ-holly-holm-vs-aspen-ladd-removed-ufc-235|title=Ex-champ Holly Holm vs. Aspen Ladd removed from UFC 235|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2019-01-31|access-date=2019-02-01}}\n\n[[Thomas Almeida]] was expected to face [[Marlon Vera]] at the event. Almeida pulled out of the fight on January 31 citing an injury.{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/in-navolging-van-holm-vs-ladd-nu-ook-thomas-almeida-vs-marlon-vera-gecanceld/|title=Following Holm vs. Ladd now also Thomas Almeida vs. Marlon Vera canceled|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2019-01-31|access-date=2019-02-01|language=nl}} He was replaced by [[Frankie Saenz]].{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/frankie-saenz-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-tegen-marlon-vera-tijdens-ufc-235-in-las-vegas/|title=Frankie Saenz takes a short notice battle against Marlon Vera during UFC 235 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2019-02-02|access-date=2019-02-02|language=nl}} However, the fight was cancelled entirely on February 27 when Vera pulled out of the fight due to illness.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/vera-vs-saenz-scratched-ufc-235|title=Vera vs. Saenz scratched from UFC 235|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2019-02-27|access-date=2019-02-27}}\n\nFormer interim title challenger [[Ovince Saint Preux]] was expected to face [[Misha Cirkunov]] at the event. [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on January 19, 2019, at the [[Barclays Center]] in [[Brooklyn]], [[New York City|New York]].{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/ufc-announces-2019-first-quarter-schedule-espn-debut|title=UFC announces 2019 first quarter schedule: ESPN debut Jan. 19 in Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-04|access-date=2018-11-04}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first in the promotion's five-year contract with [[ESPN]] and the first to be broadcast on the network's fledgling streaming service [[ESPN+]], which was launched less than a month after the initial announcement in April 2018.{{Cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/23581729/espn-ufc-reach-five-year-television-rights-deal|title=ESPN to broadcast 30 UFC events per year during 5-year deal|work=espn.com|author=ESPN Newswire|date=2018-05-23|access-date=2018-09-05}} It generated 525,000 new subscribers to the service on the day of the event alone.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2019/1/21/18191215/ufc-espn-debut-525000-new-subscribers-saturday-night-mma-news|title=UFC's ESPN+ debut pulls in 525,000 new subscribers on Saturday night|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2019-01-21|access-date=2019-01-22|archive-date=2019-01-22|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190122094906/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2019/1/21/18191215/ufc-espn-debut-525000-new-subscribers-saturday-night-mma-news|url-status=dead}} It was also the first UFC event to showcase the new UFC legacy belt, which was given out to the winner of ''Cejudo vs. Dillashaw''.{{Cite web |date=2019-01-18 |title=At long last, UFC on ESPN+ 1 unveils the new UFC Legacy Belt |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2019/01/ufc-on-espn-plus-1-unveils-new-ufc-legacy-belt-brooklyn-cejudo-dillashaw-title |access-date=2023-02-05 |website=mmajunkie.usatoday.com |language=en-US}}\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 350\n | image1 = Henry Cejudo.jpg\n | width1 = 2500 | height1 = 2000\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = TJ Dillashaw.png\n | width2 = 2500 | height2 = 2900\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer = Cejudo ''(left)'' is the first [[Summer Olympics|Olympic]] champion to become a UFC champion. Meanwhile, Dillashaw ''(right)'' the first challenger in a champion vs. champion bout to drop a division.\n}}\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 55 kg|2008 Olympic gold medalist in freestyle wrestling]] and current champion [[Henry Cejudo]] and current two-time [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[T.J. Dillashaw]] headlined this event.{{Cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Henry-Cejudo-vs-TJ-Dillashaw-Moved-from-UFC-233-to-Headline-UFC-Debut-on-ESPN43-146595|title=Henry Cejudo vs. T.J. Dillashaw moved from UFC 233 to headline UFC debut on ESPN+|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2018-12-05|access-date=2018-12-06}} If successful, Dillashaw would become the third man to be champion in two divisions simultaneously and fourth fighter overall (after [[Conor McGregor]] at [[UFC 205]], [[Daniel Cormier]] at [[UFC 226]] and [[Amanda Nunes]] at [[UFC 232]]), as well as the seventh person overall to win a title in different divisions. This was the fifth time in UFC history that champions in different divisions fought for the same title, following [[UFC 94]], [[UFC 205]], [[UFC 226]] and [[UFC 232]]. The pairing was initially expected to take place at [[UFC 233]] a week later, but was moved on December 5.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/henry-cejudo-vs-tj-dillashaw-title-fight-official-ufc-233-anaheim|title=Henry Cejudo vs. T.J. Dillashaw flyweight title fight official for UFC 233 in Anaheim|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-26|access-date=2018-11-27}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Rachael Ostovich]] and [[Paige VanZant]] was originally canceled on November 20 due to Ostovich being assaulted by her husband, resulting in a broken orbital bone.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmanytt.com/ufc/rachael-ostovich-ruled-out-of-her-fight-against-paige-vanzant/|title=Rachael Ostovich ruled out of her fight against Paige VanZant|publisher=mmanytt.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2018-11-20|access-date=2018-11-20}} However, after getting a second opinion from a doctor, though, Ostovich got the green light to stay in the bout.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/rachael-ostovich-vs-paige-vanzant-back-on-ufc-espn-1-second-opinion-assault|title=Second opinion for Rachael Ostovich puts fight with Paige VanZant back on at UFC on ESPN+ 1|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-27|access-date=2018-11-29}} After announcing that former [[NFL]] player [[Greg Hardy]] would make his UFC debut at this event, the decision to put him on the same card as Ostovich was met with backlash by many people as Hardy was found guilty of assaulting an ex-girlfriend, and sentenced to 18 months probation as well as being suspended for 10 games by the NFL.{{Cite news|url=https://www.wsoctv.com/news/local/panthers-hardy-trial-start-tuesday/113293416|title=Panthers' Greg Hardy found guilty on 2 counts of domestic violence|publisher=wsoctv.com|author=Jim Bradley |date=2014-07-14|access-date=2018-12-07}}{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/12/6/18128476/ufc-shows-spectacular-misjudgment-placing-greg-hardy-on-same-card-as-rachael-ostovich|title=UFC shows spectacular misjudgment placing Greg Hardy on same card as Rachael Ostovich|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Chiappetta|date=2018-12-06|access-date=2018-12-06}}{{Cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Greg-Hardy-to-Share-Card-with-Rachael-Ostovich-for-UFC-Debut-in-Brooklyn-on-Jan-19-146581|title=Greg Hardy to share card with Rachael Ostovich for UFC debut in Brooklyn on Jan. 19|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2018-12-05|access-date=2018-12-05}} The charges and suspension were later dropped after the victim failed to appear in court to testify, but Hardy's arrest and trial were the focus of considerable controversy.{{Cite news|url=http://www.wbtv.com/story/28059971/charges-dismissed-in-greg-hardy-trial-after-victim-fails-to-show-for-court/?clienttype=generic&mobilecgbypass|title=Panthers: No change in Greg Hardy status after charges dismissed\n|publisher=wbtv.com|author=Staff|date=2015-02-09|access-date=2018-12-06}} UFC president [[Dana White]] later claimed that Ostovich was \"totally cool\" with Hardy on the same card as her.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/dana-white-rachael-ostovich-totally-cool-greg-hardy-same-card-ufc-espn-1-brooklyn-domestic-violence|title=Dana White: Rachael Ostovich news|url=http://www.espn.com/espnw/sports/article/25216580/joanne-calderwood-ariane-lipski-flyweight-fight-added-ufc-233|title=Joanne Calderwood-Ariane Lipski flyweight fight added to UFC news|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc-233/ariane-lipski-vs-joanne-calderwood-moved-from-ufc-233-to-ufc-brooklyn/|title=Ariane Lipski vs Joanne Calderwood moved from UFC 233 to UFC Brooklyn|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Drake Riggs|date=2018-12-03|access-date=2018-12-03}}\n\nAs a result of the cancellation of [[UFC 233]], a flyweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Joseph Benavidez]] and [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] was briefly linked to this event.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/multiple-ufc-233-fights-get-new-dates-locations-after-jan-26-card-scratched|title=Multiple UFC 233 fights get new dates, locations after Jan. 26 card scratched|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-12-12|access-date=2018-12-12}} However the following day, the promotion clarified plans indicating that the pairing was off and that Benavidez would be an alternate for the headliner bout in the event that either Cejudo or Dillashaw would be forced from the main event.{{Cite news|url=http://uk.ufc.com/news/2019-Fight-Updates|title=2019 fight updates|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2018-12-13|access-date=2018-12-13}}{{Cite Figueiredo off|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-12-13|access-date=2018-12-13}} Later it was announced that despite still being an alternate, Benavidez would face [[Dustin Ortiz]] in a rematch of his unanimous decision win at [[UFC Fight Night: Edgar vs. Swanson]] in November 2014.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/joseph-benavidez-gets-ufc-on-espn-1-fight-after-all-rematches-dustin-ortiz/|title=Joseph Benavidez gets UFC on ESPN+ 1 fight after all, rematches Dustin Ortiz|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-12-15}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Thomas Almeida]] and [[Cory Sandhagen]] was briefly linked to the event. However, on December 22, it was announced that Sandhagen would face [[John Lineker]] in the event instead.{{Cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/25590084|title=Sources: Lineker-Sandhagen in works for Jan. 19|date=2018-12-22|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|access-date=2018-12-29}} On January 10, Lineker was forced to withdraw from the bout due to a rib injury.{{cite web|url=https://m.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-John-Lineker-Injured-Out-of-Bout-with-Cory-Sandhagen-at-UFC-on-ESPN43-1-148051|date=2019-01-10|title=John Lineker out of bout with Cory Sandhagen at UFC on ESPN|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|access-date=2019-01-10}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Mario Bautista]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/mario-bautista-in-for-john-lineker-vs-cory-sandhagen-ufc-brooklyn|title=Report: Mario Bautista in for John Lineker, meets Cory Sandhagen at UFC Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2019-01-12|access-date=2019-01-12}}\n\n[[Ion Cuțelaba]] was expected to face former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Glover Teixeira]] at this event. However, on January 10, it was reported that Cuțelaba was forced out of the bout due to injury.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ion-Cutelaba-Injured-Out-of-Bout-with-Glover-Teixeira-at-UFC-Brooklyn-148055|title=Ion Cutelaba injured, out of bout with Glover Teixeira at UFC Brooklyn|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2019-01-10|access-date=2019-01-10}} He was replaced by [[Karl Roberson]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2019/1/11/18179397/karl-roberson-steps-in-to-fight-glover-teixeira-at-ufc-brooklyn|date=2019-01-11|title=Karl Roberson steps in to fight Glover Teixeira at UFC Brooklyn|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|access-date=2019-01-11}}\n\n[[Randy Brown (fighter)|Randy Brown]] was expected to face [[Chance Rencountre]], but pulled out in the week leading up to the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/randy-brown-off-ufc-on-espn-plus-1-brooklyn-despite-being-100-percent-healthy|title=Randy Brown says he's off UFC on ESPN+ 1 card in Brooklyn – despite being '100 percent healthy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2019-01-13|access-date=2019-01-14}} [[Dwight Grant]] was soon announced as his replacement, but it was later reported that he was unable to be cleared to fight due to an \"eye issue\".{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/dwight-grant-steps-in-vs-chance-rencountre-to-finalize-ufc-on-espn-1-lineup|title=Dwight Grant not cleared as replacement for UFC on ESPN+ 1 (UPDATED)|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2019-01-13|access-date=2019-01-14}} Promotional newcomer Kyle Stewart was then announced as Rencountre's new opponent.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/kyle-stewart-in-for-dwight-grant-meets-chance-rencountre-at-ufc-brooklyn|title=Kyle Stewart in for Dwight Grant, meets Chance Rencountre at UFC Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2019-01-14|access-date=2019-01-14}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Flyweight\n|[[Henry Cejudo]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[T.J. Silva]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Assunção vs. Moraes 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 144''''' or '''''UFC on ESPN+ 2''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on February 2, 2019, at Centro de Formação Olímpica do Nordeste in [[Fortaleza]], Brazil.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/10/19/18002228/ufc-fortaleza-brazil-february-2019|title=UFC returns to Fortaleza, Brazil in February|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-10-19|access-date=2018-11-12}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the third that the promotion has hosted in the state capital of [[Ceará]], following [[UFC on Fuel TV: Nogueira vs. Werdum]] in June 2013, and [[UFC Fight Night: Belfort vs. Gastelum]] in March 2017.\n\nA rematch between [[Raphael Assunção]] and former [[WSOF Bantamweight Championship|WSOF Bantamweight Champion]] [[Marlon Moraes]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-Raphael-Assuncao-Marlon-Moraes-to-Rematch-at-UFC-Fight-Night-144-146285|title=Raphael Assuncao, Marlon Moraes to rematch at UFC Fight Night 144|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2018-11-29|access-date=2018-11-29}} The pairing previously met in June 2017 at [[UFC 212]] with Assunção winning the fight via split decision.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/6/3/15735946/ufc-212-results-rafael-assuncao-spoils-marlon-moraes-ufc-debut|title=UFC 212 results: Raphael Assuncao [[Markus Perez]] and promotional newcomer [[Anthony Hernandez]] was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/multiple-ufc-233-fights-get-new-dates-locations-after-jan-26-card-scratched|title=Multiple UFC 233 fights get new dates, locations after Jan. 26 card scratched|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-12-12|access-date=2018-12-12}}\n\nA strawweight bout between [[Marina Rodriguez]] and [[Alexa Grasso]] was scheduled to take place at this event. However, it was reported on December 17 that Rodriguez pulled out of the event due to a hand injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/12/17/18144757/marina-rodriguez-injured-ufc-fortaleza-fight-alexa-grasso|title=Marina Rodriguez injured, out of UFC Fortaleza fight with Alexa Grasso|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-12-17|access-date=2018-12-18}} The pair was rescheduled to [[UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Gaethje]].{{Cite web|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/apos-estrear-no-ufc-com-empate-marina-rodriguez-encara-alexa-grasso-no-dia-30-de-marco.ghtml|title=After a draw in her UFC debut, Marina Rodriguez faces Alexa Grasso on March 30th|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=5 January 2019 |access-date=2019-01-06|language=pt}}\n\nDmitry Sosnovskiy was scheduled to face [[Junior Albini]] at the event. However Sosnovskiy pulled out of the fight in early January after undergoing surgery to correct a recent injury. Albini instead faced promotional newcomer [[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]].{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/surinamer-jairzinho-rozenstruik-treft-junior-albini-tijdens-ufc-fortaleza/|title=Surinamese Jairzinho Rose Bush meets Junior Albini during UFC Fortaleza|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2019-01-02|access-date=2019-01-02|language=nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Sarah Frota and former [[List of PFL champions|WSOF Flyweight Champion]] [[Magomed Bibulatov]] both missed the required weight for their respective fights. Frota weighed in at 123 pounds, 7 pounds over the strawweight non-title fight limit of 116. Meanwhile, Bibulatov weighed in at 127 pounds, 1 pound over the flyweight non-title fight limit of 126. Bibulatov was fined 20 percent of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Rogério Bontorin]], while Frota surrendered 40 percent of her purse to former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Lívia Renata Souza]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-on-ESPN43-2-Weighin-Results-Two-Heavy-One-Off-by-Seven-Pounds-148983|title=UFC on ESPN+ 2 weigh-in results: two heavy; one off by seven Champion]] [[Cain Velasquez]] and former title challenger [[Francis Ngannou]] served as the event headliner. This is Cain Velasquez's final match before moving to the WWE.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/francis-ngannou-vs-cain-velasquez-ufc-on-espn-1-headliner-phoenix|title=Francis Ngannou vs. ex-champ Cain Velasquez in the works for UFC on ESPN 1 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-12-13|access-date=2018-12-13}}{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/ufc-on-espn-1-lineup-set-features-10-bouts-on-espn|title=UFC on ESPN 1 lineup set, features 10 bouts on ESPN|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2019-01-15|access-date=2019-01-16}}\n\nAs a result of the cancellation of [[UFC 233]], fights between [[Cortney Casey]] and [[Cynthia Calvillo]], [[James Vick]] and [[Paul Felder]], [[Benito Lopez]] and [[Manny Bermudez]] and [[Aleksandra Albu]] and [[Emily Whitmire]] respectively, were rescheduled for this event.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/12/multiple-ufc-233-fights-get-new-dates-locations-after-jan-26-card-scratched|title=Multiple UFC 233 fights get new dates, locations after Jan. 26 card scratched|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-12-12|access-date=2018-12-12}}{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/veel-gevechten-van-gecancelde-ufc-233-card-verplaatst-naar-andere-evenementen/|title=Many battles of canceled UFC 233 card moved to other events|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-12-13|access-date=2018-12-13|language=nl}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Lauren Murphy]] and [[Ashlee Evans-Smith]] was expected to take place at the event. However, on December 19, Murphy announced she will need more time off to recover from surgery and won't make it to this event.{{Cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/12/19/18149191/lauren-murphy-withdraws-from-ufc-on-espn-fight-vs-ashlee-evans-smith-to-recover-from-surgery|title=Lauren Murphy withdraws from UFC on ESPN fight vs. Ashlee Evans-Smith to recover from surgery|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2018-12-19|access-date=2018-12-19}} On December 20, it was reported that [[Andrea Lee (fighter)|Andrea Lee]] would replace Murphy against Evans-Smith.{{Cite news|url=https://mymmanews.com/andrea-lee-vs-ashlee-evans-smith/|title=Andrea Lee in for Lauren Murphy vs. Ashlee Evans-Smith at UFC on ESPN 1|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2018-12-20|access-date=2018-12-21}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Renan Barão]], [[Manny Bermudez]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Jessica Penne]] missed the required weight for their respective fights. Barão weighed at 138 pounds and Bermudez weighed at 140 pounds, 2 pounds and 4 pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit of 136 respectively. Meanwhile, Penne weighed in at 118 pounds, 2 pounds over the strawweight non-title fight limit of 116. All three bouts were set to continue at catchweight. Barão and Penne were fined 20% of their purse and Bermudez was fined 30% of his purse which went to their opponents.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/02/ufc-on-espn-1-phoenix-francis-ngannou-cain-velasquez-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC on ESPN 1 weigh-in results: Three fighters miss, but all fights set|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2019-02-16|access-date=2019-02-17}}\n\nIn turn, Penne pulled out of her bout against [[Jodie Esquibel]], as she severely sprained her ankle while warming up the morning of the event. The bout was subsequently scrapped.{{Cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/espn/now?nowId=1-26018912|title=Tonight's Jodie Esquibel vs. Jessica Penne fight has been canceled|publisher=espn.com|author=Ariel headliner.{{Cite news|url=http://uk.ufc.com/news/Blachowicz-vs-Santos-added-as-5-Round-Bout-to-ESPN--3|title=Blachowicz vs. Santos added as 5 round bout to ESPN+ 3|publisher=ufc.com|author=Ryan Ferreira and Abreu were fined 20% of their purses, which went to their opponents [[Rustam Khabilov]] and [[Magomed Ankalaev]].{{Cite Santos]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Thompson vs. Pettis]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Till vs. Masvidal''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 147''''' or '''''UFC on ESPN+ 5''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on March 16, 2019 at [[The O2 Arena|The O2 Arena]] in [[London]], England.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2018/11/ufc-announces-2019-first-quarter-schedule-espn-debut|title=UFC announces 2019 first quarter schedule: ESPN debut Jan. 19 in Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-04|access-date=2018-11-04}}{{Cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/46075811|title=UFC set to return to London's O2 Arena on 16 March 2019|publisher=bbc.com|author=Staff|date=2018-11-02|access-date=2018-11-04}}\n\n==Background==\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Darren Till]] and [[Jorge Masvidal]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/25778762/darren-meet-jorge-masvidal-ufc-fight-night-march-16|title=Darren Till, Jorge Masvidal to meet in main event of UFC Fight Night|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2019-01-16|work=espn.com|access-date=2019-01-16}}\n\n[[Alessio Di Chirico]] was expected to face [[Tom Breese]] at the event. However, Di Chirico pulled out of the fight in early January citing an undisclosed injury and subsequent surgery.{{Cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Alessio-Di-Chirico-Withdraws-from-UFC-Fight-Night-London-Bout-vs-Tom-Breese-147739|title=Alessio Di Chirico withdraws from UFC Fight Night London bout vs. Tom Breese|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tudor Leonte|date=2018-01-02|access-date=2019-01-02}} On January 8, it was reported that [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil]] middleweight winner [[Cezar Ferreira]] would be his replacement. Ferreira and Breese were expected to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Magny vs. Ponzinibbio]] in November 2018, but Breese pulled out due to injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/1/8/18173568/tom-breese-gets-new-ufc-london-assignment-as-cezar-ferreria-replaces-alessio-di-chirico|title=Tom Breese gets new UFC London assignment as Cezar Ferreria replaces Alessio Di Chirico|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Peter Carroll|date=2018-01-08|access-date=2019-01-08}} In turn, Ferreira withdrew from the bout on February 1, citing a knee injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/2/1/18207546/cezar-ferreira-announces-withdrawal-from-ufc-london|title=Cezar Ferreira announces withdrawal from UFC London|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=MMA Fighting Newswire|date=2019-02-01|access-date=2019-02-02}} Breese was expected to face [[Ian Heinisch]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/2/7/18215427/ian-heinisch-steps-in-to-fight-tom-breese-at-ufc-london|title=Ian Heinisch steps in to fight Tom Breese at UFC London|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2019-02-07|access-date=2019-02-07}} However, the fight was cancelled on the day of the event due to undisclosed health issues for Breese.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/3/16/18268543/tom-breese-withdraws-from-ufc-london-bout-against-ian-heinisch|title=Tom Breese withdraws from UFC London bout against Ian Heinisch|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Peter Caroll|date=2019-03-16|access-date=2019-03-16}}\n\n[[Gökhan Saki]] was scheduled to face [[Saparbek Safarov]] at the event. However Saki pulled out of the fight in late February due to an undisclosed injury.{{Cite news|url=https://mmadna.nl/gokhan-saki-geblesseerd-vecht-niet-in-londen/|title=Gökhan Saki injured, will not fight in London|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2019-02-28|access-date=2019-02-28|language=nl}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Nicolae Negumereanu]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/nick-negumereanu-maakt-ufc-debuut-tegen-saparbeg-saforov-tijdens-ufc-londen/|title=Nick Negumereanu makes UFC debut against Saparbek Saforov during UFC London|publisher=mmadna.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2019-03-04|access-date=2019-03-04|language=nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Jack Marshman]] weighed in at 188 lb, 2 pounds over the middleweight non-title fight limit of 186 lb. He was fined 20% of his fight purse and his bout against [[John Phillips (fighter)|John Phillips]] proceeded at catchweight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmaweekly.com/ufc-london-weigh-in-results-till-vs-masvidal-set-one-main-card-fighter-misses-weight|title=UFC London weigh-in results: Till vs. Masvidal set, one main card fighter misses weight|date=2019-03-15|publisher=mmaweekly.com|author=Staff|access-date=2019-03-15}}\n\nDuring the main card broadcast, the UFC announced that former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Michael Bisping]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter 3]] light heavyweight winner) will be inducted in the UFC's Hall of Fame during \"International Fight Week\" in July, a day before [[UFC 239]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/3/16/18268887/michael-bisping-to-be-inducted-into-ufc-hall-of-fame|title=Michael Bisping to be inducted into UFC Hall of Fame|date=2019-03-16|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|access-date=2019-03-16|archive-date=4 April 2019|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190404031828/https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2019/3/16/18268887/michael-bisping-to-be-inducted-into-ufc-hall-of-fame|url-status=dead}}\n\n===Post-Fight Altercation===\nThe event was marked by a brief backstage altercation between [[Jorge Masvidal]] and [[Leon Edwards]] as Masvidal was conducting a post-fight interview with [[ESPN]] reporter [[Laura Sanko]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.usatoday.com/story/sports/mma/2019/03/17/jorge-masvidal-says-leon-edwards-put-his-hands-up-before-punches-thrown-backstage-at-ufc-london/39214801/|title=Jorge Masvidal: Leon Edwards 'put his hands up' before punches thrown backstage in London|date=2019-03-17|publisher=USA Today|author=Simon Samano|access-date=2019-03-19}} The altercation, broadcast by ESPN resulted in an open cut under the left eye of Edwards and received coverage under specialized media outlets. In posterior interviews, each of the fighters have diverging accounts of the events.{{Cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/26299136/edwards-disputes-masvidal-account-melee|title=Edwards disputes Masvidal's account of melee|date=2019-03-19|publisher=ESPN|author=Greg Rosenstein|access-date=2019-03-19}} Edwards declined to press charges. UFC President [[Dana White]] did not manifest sanctions to either of the fighters, however has blamed his own staff.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2019/3/19/18273287/jorge-masvidal-punishment-backstage-leon-edwards-fine-suspension-espn-plus-5-ufc-london-mma|title=Dana White won't punish Jorge Masvidal for backstage brawl with Leon Edwards – 'I blame my staff'|date=2019-03-19|publisher=MMAMania|author=Adam Guillen Jr.|access-date=2019-06-16}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]] and former [[WEC Lightweight Championship|WEC]] and [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Anthony Pettis]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite news|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/25771565/anthony-pettis-face-stephen-thompson-move-welterweight|title=Anthony Pettis will fight Stephen Thompson in UFC welterweight However, it was reported that Day was pulled from the bout due to injury and he was replaced by Ryan MacDonald.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/report-ryan-macdonald-replaces-martin-day-ufc-fight-night-nashville,%20/mma/report-ryan-macdonald-replaces-martin-day-ufc-fight-night-nashville|title=Report: Ryan MacDonald replaces Martin Day at UFC Fight Night Nashville|publisher=fightful.com|author=David Tees|date=2019-02-27|access-date=2019-02-27}}\n\n[[Marlon Vera]] was expected to face [[Frankie Saenz]] in a bantamweight bout at [[UFC 235]]. However, the bout was cancelled on fight week when Vera pulled out due to illness.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/vera-vs-saenz-scratched-ufc-235|title=Vera vs. Saenz scratched from UFC 235|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2019-02-27|access-date=2019-02-27}} The bout was rescheduled and eventually took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/marlon-vera-vs-frankie-saenz-opnieuw-ingepland-ditmaal-ufc-nashville/|title=Marlon Vera vs. Frankie Saenz again scheduled, this time UFC Nashville|publisher=mmadna.com|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2019-03-04|access-date=2019-03-04|language=nl}}\n\n[[Nasrat Haqparast]] and [[Chris Gruetzemacher]] pulled out of their respective bouts, due to injury, against [[John Makdessi]] and [[Jesus Pinedo]]. In turn, Makdessi and Pinedo ended up facing each other.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/mma/canadian-john-makdessi-gets-new-opponent-ufc-card-nashville/sn-amp/|title=Canadian John Makdessi gets new opponent for UFC card in Nashville|publisher=sports net.ca|author=Canadian Press|date=2019-03-13|access-date=2019-03-13}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Luis Peña (fighter)|Luis Peña]] weighed in at 148.5 lb, 2.5 pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit of 146 lb. He was fined 30% of his fight purse and his bout against [[Steven Peterson]] proceeded at catchweight.{{Cite Holloway vs. Poirier 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Gaethje''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 2''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on March 30, 2019 at the [[Wells Fargo Center (Philadelphia)|Wells Fargo Center]] in [[Philadelphia, Pennsylvania]].{{Cite web|url=http://www.philly.com/sports/ufc-fight-night-philadelphia-wells-fargo-center-20190108.html|title=UFC Fight Night coming to Wells Fargo Center in March|publisher=philly.com|author=Katie McInerney|date=2019-01-08|access-date=2019-01-08}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the third UFC event in Philadelphia and the first since [[UFC 133]] in August 2011.\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Edson Barboza]] and former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]] [[Justin Gaethje]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/barboza-vs-gaethje-headline-philadelphia-event|title=Barboza vs. Gaethje to headline Philadelphia event|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2019-01-22|access-date=2019-01-23}}{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/01/edson-barboza-vs-justin-gaethje-official-for-ufc-on-espn-2-headliner|title=Edson Barboza vs. Justin Gaethje official for UFC on ESPN 2 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2019-01-22|access-date=2019-01-22}}\n\nA strawweight bout between [[Alexa Grasso]] and [[Marina Rodriguez]] was scheduled at [[UFC Fight Night: Assunção vs. Moraes 2]]. However, it was reported on December 17, that Rodriguez pulled out of the event due to a hand injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/12/17/18144757/marina-rodriguez-injured-ufc-fortaleza-fight-alexa-grasso|title=Marina Rodriguez injured, out of UFC Fortaleza fight with Alexa Grasso|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2018-12-17|access-date=2018-12-18}} The pairing was left intact and rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/apos-estrear-no-ufc-com-empate-marina-rodriguez-encara-alexa-grasso-no-dia-30-de-marco.ghtml|title=After a draw in her debut, Marina Rodriguez faces Alexa Grasso on March 30|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2019-01-06|access-date=2019-01-06|language=pt}} In turn, it was reported that Grasso was pulled from the bout due to injury and replaced by former [[WSOF Women's Strawweight Championship|WSOF Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Jessica Aguilar]].{{Cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/jessica-aguilar-vervangt-alexa-grasso-tegen-marina-rodriguez-tijdens-ufc-philadelphia/|title=Jessica Aguilar replaces Alexa Grasso against Marina Rodriguez during UFC Philadelphiaa|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2019-03-23|access-date=2019-03-23|language=nl}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Ray Borg]] and [[Liu Pingyuan]] was scheduled to take place at the event.{{Cite news|url=https://mmadna.nl/ray-borg-gaat-naar-bantamweight-en-vecht-tegen-pingyuan-liu-tijdens-ufc-philadelphia/|title=Ray Borg goes to Bantamweight and fights Pingyuan Liu at UFC Philadelphia|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2019-02-26|access-date=2019-02-26|language=nl}} However, it was reported on March 13, that Liu was replaced by newcomer [[Kyler Phillips]] for an undisclosed reason.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/kyler-phillips-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-tegen-ray-borg-tijdens-ufc-philadelphia/|title=Kyler Phillips tackles short notice fight against Ray Borg during UFC Philadelphia|author=Marcel Dorff|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2019-03-13|access-date=2019-03-13|language=nl}} In turn, Phillips was removed from the card on March 25 for undisclosed reasons and replaced by [[Casey Kenney]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/03/lfa-double-champ-casey-kenney-vs-ray-borg-short-notice-ufc-philadelphia|title=LFA double champ Casey Kenney steps in vs. Ray Borg on short notice at UFC Philadelphia|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2019-03-25|access-date=2019-03-26}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Borg weighed in at 137.75 lb, 1.75 lb over the bantamweight non-title fight limit of 136 lb. He was fined 20% of his fight purse and his bout against Kenney proceeded at catchweight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/03/ufc-on-espn-2-philadelphia-edson-barboza-justin-gaethje-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC on ESPN 2 weigh-in results: Barboza, Gaethje official, but Ray Borg misses|date=2019-03-29|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2019-03-29}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN)}}\n{{MMAevent bout
